1	# Notes
2	
3	See [design.md](../crates/actor-x1/notes/design.md)
4	
5	
6	## jj tips
7	
8	For users new to jj see [jj-tips.md](jj-tips.md).
9	
10	```
11	## Chores format
12	
13	Filename: "chores-XX.md"
14	example: chores-01.md
15	
16	Format of section labels: "## <short description> (X.Y.Z)"
17	example: "## Topic format description (0.1.0)"
18	
19	Example chore file:
20	```
21	# Chores-01.md
22	 
23	General maintenance tasks and considerations for the project see other files for
24	more specific topics. A chore in a chores file provides quick information on the
25	how and why of a particular chore.
26	
27	## Do something (1.3.1)
28	
29	Describe something
30	```
31	
32	## Versioning during development
33	
34	This is using jujustiu, jj + git and we'll see how it goes. Below is my
35	git workflow, jj will be different but we'll have to discover that as
36	we go.
37	
38	Every plan must start with a version bump. Choose the approach based on scope:
39	
40	- **Single-step** (recommended for mechanical/focused changes): bump directly to
41	  `X.Y.Z`, implement in one commit. Simpler history.
42	- **Multi-step** (for exploratory/large changes): bump to `X.Y.Z-devN`, implement
43	  across multiple commits, final commit removes `-devN`.
44	
45	The plan should recommend one approach and get user approval before starting.
46	
47	For multi-step:
48	1. Bump version to `X.Y.Z-devN` with a plan and commit as a chore marker
49	2. Implement in one or more `-devN` commits (bump N as needed)
50	3. Final commit removes `-devN`, updates todo/chores — this is the "done" marker
51	
52	The final release commit (without `-devN`) signals completion rather than amending
53	prior commits. This keeps the git history readable and makes it easy to see which
54	commits were exploratory vs final.
55	
56	## Todo format
57	
58	Todo.md contains two main sections "Todo" and "Done" each item is a
59	short explanations of a tasks and links to more details using 1 or more
60	references.
61	
62	Multiple references must be separated: `[2],[3]` not `[2,3]` or `[2][3]`.
63	In markdown, `[2,3]` is a single ref key (won't resolve) and `[2][3]`
64	is parsed as display text `2` with ref key `3` (so `[2]` won't resolve).
65	
66	Examples:
67	
68	# Todo
69	
70	- Add new feature X [details](chores-01.md#feature-x)
71	- Fix bug Y [1]
72	
73	# Done
74	
75	- Fixed issue Z [2],[3]
76	
77	[1]: chores-01bugs.md#bug-y
78	
```

30	  `cpu_boost(Duration)` — since `calibrate()` no longer warms

33	  boost frequency first
34	- Add `--repeat N` flag to goal1/goal2 to run the full
35	  warmup+measurement sequence N times and report cross-run mean
36	  and stdev on `mean min-p99` / `adj mean min-p99`. Built-in
37	  replacement for the `for _ in 1..=30; do goal1 2 ...` shell
38	  loop; gives the empirical repeatability number A/B work
39	  depends on. See [[18]] for floors, technique, and
40	  expected-scale heuristics
41	- Revisit band-table `range` semantics — drop the `+1` so
42	  `first == last → range = 0` (true "no spread" instead of
43	  today's "1 tk / rounds to 0 ns"). `last - first` reads
44	  more naturally and matches what a reader expects "range"
45	  to mean. Pre-existing behavior, not introduced in 0.3.0-3
46	- **Bound `TProbe`'s records-buffer memory.** Each scope
47	  appends a 32-byte `Record` to an unbounded `Vec`; at
48	  `goal1 --inner 10` (~13M scopes/s), 20 s of measurement
49	  buffers 260M records ≈ 8 GB minimum, ~16 GB after
50	  `Vec` power-of-2 over-allocation, which swaps/OOMs on
51	  typical dev boxes. Pre-existing from 0.1.0-2 when the
52	  probe was vendored; becomes hard to miss at long
53	  measurement windows + small `inner`. Options (pick one
54	  once we instrument drain cost):
55	  - (a) **Hard cap + truncation counter.** Fixed max
56	    records (e.g. 64M ≈ 2 GB); past the cap, increment a
57	    dropped counter and skip the `push`. Report shows
58	    `truncated: N scopes dropped`. Preserves hot-path
59	    cost. Partial data on very long runs.
60	  - (b) **Periodic inline drain.** When records hit a
61	    threshold, drain into the histogram and continue.
62	    Memory bounded. Adds occasional drain stalls (ms-ish)
63	    that show up in the `p99-max` tail.
64	  - (c) **Inline drain, no buffer.** `end` / `end_batch`
65	    compute `per_event` and call `hist.record` directly.
66	    Fully bounded memory, no stalls, but adds ~15–20 ns
67	    to the hot path — non-trivial when the probe is
68	    measuring ~5 ns work per event.
69	  - (d) **Skinnier record (just `u64` per-event ticks).**
70	    4× memory reduction. Buys time but doesn't fix the
71	    unbounded-growth shape.
72	- Clean up `SingleThreadRuntime::run_for` inner loop:
73	  (a) remove the unnecessary `let Self { actors, queue, probe } = self;`
74	      destructure — disjoint `&mut` through named field paths

1	# Chores-03.md
2	
3	Chores for the `0.6.0` ladder onward.
4	
5	## RuntimeZC lifecycle: startup / run / stop — plan marker (0.6.0-0)
6	
7	Plan marker for the `0.6.0` multi-step ladder. Splits the
8	runtime's all-in-one `run` / `run_probed` into a caller-
9	orchestrated lifecycle (`startup` / `Handle::run` /
10	`Handle::stop`). Probe support leaves the runtime entirely;
11	re-enters later via `TProbe::Counter` or an actor-wrapper
12	trait (out of scope for this ladder). `stop` is the
13	explicit graceful primitive, matching the post-`0.6.0`
14	typed-message-to-runtime story.
15	
16	### Shape
17	
18	```
19	let handle = rt.startup(&mut mgr, initial, pin_cores);
20	handle.run(duration);        // optional, repeatable
21	let total = handle.stop();   // u64 aggregate; sends Shutdown
22	```
23	
24	### Multi-step ladder
25	
26	(Revised mid-flight after `0.6.0-1`. Original ladder had
27	three sub-steps; expanded to five to make the trait
28	introduction and amortized-bench work explicit. Revised
29	again after `0.6.0-2` to insert the shared-`report`-module
30	side-refactor as `0.6.0-3`, shifting the lifecycle work
31	that was -3/-4/-5 to -4/-5/-6.)
32	
33	- `0.6.0-0` — plan marker (this section + version bump).
34	- `0.6.0-1` — `Handle` + `RuntimeZC::startup` /
35	  `Handle::run` / `Handle::stop`; tests updated. Existing
36	  `run` / `run_probed` stay alongside (additive step).
37	- `0.6.0-2` — Migrate `goalzc` bin and `goalzc-crit` bench
38	  to the lifecycle API. New `Handle::reset_count` (sends
39	  `ClearProbe`; each actor thread zeros its per-thread
40	  count on receipt). `--warmup` restored on `goalzc` and
41	  in `goalzc-crit`'s warmup → measurement split. `goalzc`
42	  loses its band-table (no probing); keeps the msg/s line
43	  as a smoke generator.
44	- `0.6.0-3` — Shared `actor_x1::report` module. Extracts
45	  the bin-prelude helpers duplicated across `goal1` /
46	  `goal2` / `goalzc` (parser, banner, apparatus line,
47	  pinning line) into a single home so the upcoming
48	  probing-re-enters work (post-`0.6.0`, when a probed
49	  `goalzc_probed` bin lands on the wrapper-trait probe
50	  path) has a clean shared surface to call into instead
51	  of yet another copy.
52	- `0.6.0-4` — `Handle::query_count` via signal-and-reply
53	  (oneshot per thread). `goalzc-crit` goes amortized:
54	  `startup` once / `reset_count → run(duration) →
55	  query_count` per criterion sample. Reports the new
56	  amortized number; bench-vs-bin gap collapses (or
57	  doesn't, and we learn something).
58	- `0.6.0-5` — Introduce `Runtime` trait. `RuntimeZC` impls
59	  it. `goalzc` / `goalzc-crit` / tests reach through the
60	  trait, not the concrete type. `SignalZC::ClearProbe`
61	  renamed to `ClearCount` (probe is gone).
62	- `0.6.0-6` — Remove legacy `run` / `run_probed` /
63	  `actor_loop_probed`. Runtime is probe-free +
64	  lifecycle-only.
65	- `0.6.0` — close.
66	
67	### Decisions locked in
68	
69	- **Counter**: per-thread local `u64`, no atomics. Returned
70	  via `JoinHandle` at `stop`. `stop -> u64` aggregate. KIS;
71	  per-actor breakdown is a future `TProbe::Counter`
72	  concern.
73	- **`Handle::run(duration) -> ()`** — just sleeps for the
74	  run window. Counts not readable mid-flight by design.
75	- **`Handle::Drop`** calls `stop` if forgotten; result is
76	  discarded. Join errors are swallowed (drop must not
77	  panic-during-drop).
78	- **Probing leaves the runtime.** `goalzc`'s band-table
79	  goes with it. Probe re-introduction is post-`0.6.0`
80	  work, via `TProbe::Counter` or an actor-wrapper trait.
81	- **`Runtime` trait** introduced in `0.6.0-4`. Consumers
82	  reach through the trait so the post-`0.6.0`
83	  ActorManager-driven graceful shutdown work has a clean
84	  seam to drop into.
85	
86	### Defaults that aren't yet locked
87	
88	- `Handle::pool()` accessor so the caller can inject ad-hoc
89	  messages post-startup. Default yes; revisit during
90	  `0.6.0-1` if the API gets ugly.
91	
92	### Naming: teardown → stop
93	
94	The `0.5.0` close section's "What's next" pointer used
95	the name `teardown` for the lifecycle's shutdown
96	primitive. During the `0.6.0-0` planning round the name
97	settled on `stop` — matches the post-`0.6.0` typed-
98	message-to-runtime story. The chores-02 mention of
99	`teardown` is historical (accurate to the moment it was
100	written, pre-rename) and is left in place per the same
101	precedent `0.5.1` set with `run_no_probe → run`.
102	
103	## Handle + RuntimeZC::startup / Handle::run / Handle::stop (0.6.0-1)
104	
105	Adds the lifecycle API alongside the existing all-in-one
106	`run` / `run_probed`. Caller drives the actor mesh through
107	[`startup`] → [`Handle::run`] (any number of windows) →
108	[`Handle::stop`] (returns the aggregate `u64` count and
109	joins). Probe-free; counts come from per-thread local
110	`u64`s summed via `JoinHandle` at `stop`. The all-in-one
111	paths still work (refactored to share spawn / seed logic);
112	they go away in `0.6.0-3`.
113	
114	- `crates/actor-x1/Cargo.toml`: `0.6.0-0` → `0.6.0-1`.
115	- `crates/actor-x1/src/runtime_zc.rs`:
116	  - New private `RuntimeZC::spawn_and_seed<R>` extracts
117	    the spawn + channel-wire + initial-message-deliver
118	    logic shared by the all-in-one `run_inner` and the
119	    new `startup`. `run_inner` is now a thin wrapper that
120	    composes `spawn_and_seed` with the warmup / clear /
121	    measurement / shutdown / join sequence.
122	  - New `pub fn RuntimeZC::startup(mgr, initial, pin) ->
123	    Handle<S>` returns a live handle to the actor mesh.
124	  - New `pub struct Handle<S = MutexLifo>` with
125	    `pub fn run(&self, window: Duration)` (just sleeps),
126	    `pub fn pool(&self) -> &Pool<S>` (caller injection /
127	    inspection), `pub fn stop(self) -> u64` (sends
128	    `Shutdown`, joins, sums per-thread counts — panics
129	    on actor panic).
130	  - `impl Drop for Handle` performs silent graceful
131	    shutdown if `stop` was not called: sends `Shutdown`,
132	    joins, swallows panics and counts. Drop must not
133	    panic-during-drop.
134	  - Module-level docstring: rewritten around the two API
135	    styles (Lifecycle preferred / All-in-one legacy).
136	  - Tests: `lifecycle_round_trip_returns_total`,
137	    `lifecycle_multiple_run_windows_accumulate`,
138	    `lifecycle_handle_pool_accessor`,
139	    `lifecycle_handle_drop_shuts_down_cleanly`. All four
140	    validate the drain invariant (`pool.free_len() ==
141	    pool.size()` after the handle goes away).
142	- `notes/todo.md`: mark `0.6.0-1` done.
143	- `notes/chores-03.md`: this section.
144	
145	### Design notes
146	
147	- **`Handle`'s state**: `senders: Option<Vec<Sender<...>>>`
148	  and `handles: Option<Vec<JoinHandle<u64>>>`. `stop`
149	  consumes via `take()`; `Drop` checks the same `Option`s
150	  and runs the silent path only if `stop` didn't.
151	  Simpler than typestate; KIS.
152	- **Counter aggregation**: each actor thread keeps a
153	  local `u64`, returned via `JoinHandle::Output`. `stop`
154	  sums them with `saturating_add`. No atomics in the hot
155	  path.
156	- **No mid-flight count read**: `Handle::run(window)`
157	  returns `()`. Per-window deltas would require atomics
158	  or signal-and-reply; both rejected as not worth the
159	  complexity at the user's KIS preference.
160	- **`spawn_and_seed` lives on `RuntimeZC`**, not as a
161	  free function, because it uses `self.pool` and is the
162	  natural place for the shared logic. Generic over `R`
163	  so both probe-free and probed actor loops share it.
164	- **Pool sizing in tests**: the lifecycle tests use the
165	  `Pool::new(., 4)` ping-pong-steady-state recommendation
166	  from the `0.5.0` chores (4 buffers covers 2 in flight
167	  + 2 headroom). One initial test draft used the
168	  capacity-2 pool from a non-running test and hit
169	  `NoMsgs` under traffic; standardized on 4.
170	
171	### What's not in 0.6.0-1
172	
173	- `goalzc` and `goalzc-crit` still call the legacy
174	  `run_probed` / `run` paths. `0.6.0-2` migrates them.
175	- Legacy `RuntimeZC::run` / `run_probed` /
176	  `actor_loop_probed` / the `ClearProbe` signal path stay
177	  untouched for now. `0.6.0-3` removes them.
178	
179	## Migrate goalzc bin and goalzc-crit bench to lifecycle (0.6.0-2)
180	
181	`goalzc` and `goalzc-crit` move off the legacy all-in-one
182	`RuntimeZC::run_probed` / `run` to the lifecycle API
183	(`startup` / `Handle::run` / `Handle::stop`). Adds
184	`Handle::reset_count` so the warmup → measurement
185	boundary can zero per-thread counts mid-flight. `goalzc`
186	loses its band-table report (no probing in the runtime;
187	re-enters post-`0.6.0`) and becomes a smoke generator
188	that prints msg/s. `goalzc-crit` keeps its
189	cycle-per-sample shape, just through the new API.
190	
191	- `crates/actor-x1/Cargo.toml`: `0.6.0-1` → `0.6.0-2`.
192	- `crates/actor-x1/src/runtime_zc.rs`:
193	  - New `pub fn Handle::reset_count(&self)` sends
194	    `SignalZC::ClearProbe` to every actor thread; on
195	    receipt each thread zeros its per-thread count.
196	    Best-effort: if a thread already exited, the send
197	    silently fails (its count is already captured in
198	    its `JoinHandle::Output`).
199	  - New test `lifecycle_reset_count_runs_clean` exercises
200	    `startup → run → reset_count → run → stop`; asserts
201	    nonzero post-reset total + drain invariant.
202	- `crates/actor-x1/src/bin/goalzc.rs`:
203	  - CLI: drop `--ticks` (`-t`), `--decimals` (`-d`).
204	    Keep `duration_s` (positional), `--warmup` (`-w`,
205	    default 0.5), `--size` (`-s`), `--pin` (`-p`).
206	  - Drop the apparatus calibration call and the
207	    per-thread `tprobe` band-table report.
208	  - Use `rt.startup` → `handle.run(warmup)` →
209	    `handle.reset_count()` → `handle.run(measurement)`
210	    → `handle.stop` to drive the actors.
211	  - Output: version banner, throughput line
212	    (`goalzc: <count> messages in <Ds> (<R> M msg/s,
213	    <N> actors, size=<S> B)`), and `pinning:` line if
214	    `--pin` was set. No `apparatus:` line, no band
215	    table.
216	  - Imports trimmed: `tprobe::{fmt_commas, pin}` only
217	    (was `tprobe::{self as perf, fmt_commas, pin,
218	    ticks}` plus probe-side report calls).
219	  - `n_actors = 2` hardcoded since the bin always
220	    creates two actors (assertion already pins
221	    `(a_id, b_id) == (0, 1)`).
222	- `crates/actor-x1/benches/goalzc-crit.rs`:
223	  - Switch `rt.run` → `rt.startup` /
224	    `handle.run(warmup)` / `handle.reset_count()` /
225	    `handle.run(measurement)` / `handle.stop`.
226	  - Keep the prior 50 ms warmup + 100 ms measurement
227	    split. `per_msg_ns = measurement / measurement_count`
228	    (post-reset only).
229	  - Module docstring rewritten around the lifecycle
230	    API and the smoke-bench scope.
231	- `notes/todo.md`: mark `0.6.0-2` done.
232	- `notes/chores-03.md`: this section.
233	
234	### Smoke results (`--size 64`, unpinned)
235	
236	- `goalzc-crit/pingpong`: ~260 K msg/s (criterion CI
237	  [250, 269] K elem/s; "Performance has improved",
238	  p=0.04 vs the pre-restoration single-window baseline
239	  at ~233 K). Restoring `--warmup` (50 ms) before the
240	  100 ms measurement window — and using `reset_count`
241	  to drop the warmup count — exposes the steady-state
242	  rate.
243	- `goalzc 10 -w 3`: ~293 K msg/s. Modest improvement vs
244	  the pre-`0.6.0` probed path (~281 K); the bot thinks
245	  this is the probe overhead leaving the hot loop —
246	  ~5.8 ns / dispatch ≈ 0.17% of the per-message cost —
247	  near the edge of run-to-run noise but pointing the
248	  right way.
249	- Bench-vs-bin ratio: 260 / 293 ≈ 0.89, narrower than
250	  the pre-restoration 232 / 281 ≈ 0.83. The remaining
251	  11% is the per-sample fresh-`startup` cost in the
252	  bench; `0.6.0-3`'s `startup`-once + `query_count`
253	  amortization aims at that.
254	
255	### CLI breaking change in 0.6.0-2
256	
257	`goalzc`'s `--ticks` / `--decimals` flags are gone (band-
258	table is gone). `--warmup` is preserved with the same
259	semantics it had pre-`0.6.0`: warmup window precedes
260	measurement, and reported throughput reflects only the
261	measurement window.
262	
263	### Naming: window → duration
264	
265	`Handle::run`'s parameter `window: Duration` renamed to
266	`duration: Duration` — matches `std::thread::sleep(dur)`
267	and `tokio::time::sleep(duration)` conventions, removes
268	the overloaded "window" reading from the public API. In
269	prose, bare "window" becomes "run window"; "warmup
270	window" / "measurement window" stay as already-qualified
271	compound terms. The `0.6.0-1` chores section above
272	describes the parameter as `window: Duration`, accurate
273	at the time, left in place per the same precedent
274	`0.5.1` set with `run_no_probe → run`.
275	
276	## Shared `report` module: extract bin-prelude helpers (0.6.0-3)
277	
278	Side-refactor inserted into the `0.6.0` ladder between the
279	lifecycle migration (`0.6.0-2`) and the upcoming
280	`query_count` work (`0.6.0-4`). Collapses the prelude
281	helpers that `goal1` / `goal2` / `goalzc` each had a copy
282	of into a single `actor_x1::report` module. No behavior
283	change — output is byte-for-byte identical across all three
284	bins.
285	
286	Motivation:
287	
288	- The bot thinks the post-`0.6.0` probing-re-enters work
289	  (when `goalzc_probed` lands on the wrapper-trait / 
290	  `TProbe::Counter` path) will want the same prelude lines
291	  as `goal1` / `goal2`. Doing the dedup *before* that bin
292	  arrives means one shared surface to call into instead
293	  of a fourth copy.
294	- `goal1` and `goal2` each duplicated the
295	  `parse_non_negative_secs` clap helper, the
296	  `framing / loop_per_iter / per-event` apparatus
297	  printout, and the `pinning:` line; `goalzc` had the
298	  parser and the multi-actor pinning printout.
299	
300	Edits:
301	
302	- `crates/actor-x1/Cargo.toml`: `0.6.0-2` → `0.6.0-3`.
303	- `crates/actor-x1/src/lib.rs`: declare `pub mod report;`.
304	- `crates/actor-x1/src/report.rs`: new module.
305	  - `parse_non_negative_secs(s) -> Result<f64, String>` —
306	    clap `value_parser` for non-negative finite seconds.
307	  - `parse_pin_cores(spec: Option<&str>) -> Vec<usize>` —
308	    `None` → `vec![]`; bad input prints
309	    `error: --pin: <msg>` and exits `2`. Centralizing the
310	    exit here keeps each bin free of duplicated error
311	    handling.
312	  - `print_banner()` — `actor-x1 <version>` one-liner from
313	    this crate's `CARGO_PKG_*`.
314	  - `print_apparatus(&Overhead, inner: u64)` — the
315	    `apparatus: framing=... ; loop_per_iter=... ; per-event
316	    at inner=N = ...` line.
317	  - `print_pinning(&[usize], n_actors: usize)` — multi-actor
318	    `pinning:` line (goal2 / goalzc wording —
319	    `actor<i>→core<n>`).
320	  - `print_pinning_main(Option<usize>)` — single-thread
321	    `pinning:` line (goal1 wording — `main → core <c>`).
322	    Distinct from `print_pinning` because goal1 pins the
323	    main thread itself, not a spawned actor; the prior
324	    output preserved both forms verbatim.
325	- `crates/actor-x1/src/bin/goal1.rs`: strip duplicated
326	  helpers; call `report::print_banner`,
327	  `report::parse_pin_cores`, `report::print_pinning_main`,
328	  `report::print_apparatus`. `parse_non_negative_secs`
329	  flag bodies now reference `report::parse_non_negative_secs`.
330	  Tprobe import trimmed: `tprobe::{self as perf, fmt_commas,
331	  pin}` (drops `ticks` — the per-event ns conversion lives
332	  in `report` now).
333	- `crates/actor-x1/src/bin/goal2.rs`: same shape.
334	  `parse_pin_cores` collapses the `match cli.pin.as_deref()
335	  { ... pin::parse_cores ... exit(2) }` block to one line.
336	  Tprobe import trimmed: `tprobe::{self as perf, fmt_commas}`
337	  (drops `pin` and `ticks` — goal2 doesn't pin the main
338	  thread, only spawned actors via the runtime).
339	- `crates/actor-x1/src/bin/goalzc.rs`: same shape, smaller
340	  surface (no apparatus call; no probing). Local
341	  `parse_positive_size` for `--size` stays in the bin —
342	  only goalzc has a payload-size flag, so it doesn't belong
343	  in the shared module. Tprobe import trimmed: `tprobe::
344	  fmt_commas` (drops `pin` — `parse_pin_cores` covers the
345	  one call site).
346	- `notes/todo.md`: insert `0.6.0-3` done line; renumber the
347	  lifecycle steps that were -3/-4/-5 to -4/-5/-6.
348	- `notes/chores-03.md`: revise the ladder table; this
349	  section.
350	
351	### Why the helpers live in `actor-x1`, not `tprobe`
352	
353	- The formatted lines are *bin prelude*, not perf-library
354	  surface. `tprobe` already owns the data
355	  ([`Overhead`], `ticks_per_ns`, `fmt_commas`); the bins
356	  own the *wording* (column spacing, label choice,
357	  pinning prose). Keeping the wording in `actor-x1` puts
358	  the prose next to the bins that produce it.
359	- A future external consumer of `tprobe` would print the
360	  same numbers in its own house style; promoting our
361	  particular phrasing to `tprobe` would foist our prose
362	  on every downstream user.
363	
364	### Why two pinning helpers
365	
366	`goal1` pins the main thread itself (single-threaded) and
367	its line says `pinning: main → core <c>`; `goal2` /
368	`goalzc` pin spawned actor threads and their line says
369	`pinning: actor<i>→core<n>`. The two are intentionally
370	worded differently (`main` vs `actor<i>`; spaced `→` vs
371	unspaced `→`). Preserving byte-for-byte output across the
372	refactor meant providing both forms rather than collapsing
373	them.
374	
375	### Bin-output policy left in the bins
376	
377	- `--ticks` / `--decimals` flags (probed bins only).
378	- The per-actor `probe.report(...)` call (each bin chooses
379	  which probes to render).
380	- The throughput line (`goal1: ... messages in ...`) — the
381	  bin name is baked into the format string. Promoting that
382	  to `report::print_throughput(&str, count, dur_s, ...)`
383	  would gain little and lose the literal-grep-ability of
384	  the per-bin line.
385

1	# actor-x1
2	
3	## Overview
4	
5	Actor-x1 is a system that uses Communicating
6	Sequential Process (CSP) invented by Tony Hoare
7	In 1978 and is commonly termed the actor model.
8	
9	 - There will be a management system (MS) that:
10	
11	   - Provides the capability to interact with
12	     the runtime providing control and to capture
13	     logs and assists in developing the app
14	   - Creates an hello world scaffolding
15	   - provides discovery capabilities of other
16	     other actors.
17	
18	 - A runtime that drives the system and provides
19	   resources and services for the system
20	   - interfaces between the manager and the application
21	 
22	 - One or more actors running on one or more
23	   threads. Each actor adheres to these
24	   invariants:
25	   - Has on basic entry point; handler()
26	   - The handler() handles one message at a time and
27	     must run to completion as quickly as possible.
28	   - The handler() never ever blocks
29	   - If a handler() has tasks that take to much time
30	     a technique that can be used is to spawn
31	     threads to do work. That thread would then use
32	     messages to communicate with its parent and
33	     other actors as it does its work.
34	
35	## Future capabilities
36	   - I would like Actors to be implemented as an HSM.
37	     This potentially allows an Actor to implement
38	     sophisticated algorithms and prove correct operation.
39	
40	## Stage1 runtime
41	
42	Design and implement the simplest runtime possible
43	
44	### Message
45	
46	struct Message {}
47	
48	### Actor
49	
50	Actor implements an Actor trait
51	
52	trait Actor {
53	  // Handles a message 
54	  fn handle_message(msg: Message)
55	
56	  // other methods..
57	}
58	
59	### Goal1
60	
61	- Run two actors on one thread.
62	- A single message which is an empty struct
63	- Message ping pongs between the two actors
64	  for duration in seconds specified as a f64
65	  in seconds from the command line
66	
67	## Goal2
68	
69	- Same as Goal1 but each actor on a separate thread.
70	
71	## Stage2 runtime
72	
73	We will design and implement the slightly more capable runtime
74	
75	It supports 3 actors A, B and C
76	
77	### Message
78	
79	struct Message {
80	  src_id: u32,
```

Acquainted. Quick lay of the land:

**Repo**: `actor-x1` — Rust experiment in CSP/actor-model (Hoare 1978).

**Layout**:
- `crates/actor-x1` — runtime + bins (`goal1`, `goal2`, `goalzc`) + benches
- `crates/tprobe` — perf probing (extracted in 0.2.0)
- Two jj repos: app root + `.claude` bot-session repo

**Current state**:
- Branch: `main` @ `22d569db` (clean, empty working copy on top)
- Version: `0.6.0-3` — refactor: shared report module for bin prelude
- Mid-ladder: `0.6.0` RuntimeZC lifecycle (startup/run/stop)

**0.6.0 ladder progress** (notes/todo.md:7-16):

1	//! Multi-threaded zerocopy runtime — message-moving
2	//! infrastructure.
3	//!
4	//! Pairs with [`crate::actor_manager`], which owns the actor
5	//! model surface ([`ActorZC`], [`ContextZC`], [`ActorManager`]).
6	//! This module is one consumer of that surface; future
7	//! transports (M:N scheduler, alternative channel types, …)
8	//! would be peers.
9	//!
10	//! Two API styles in this version:
11	//!
12	//! - **Lifecycle (preferred, new in `0.6.0-1`)**: caller
13	//!   composes [`RuntimeZC::startup`] →
14	//!   [`Handle::run`] (any number of run windows) →
15	//!   [`Handle::stop`]. `stop` returns the aggregate `u64`
16	//!   message count across all actor threads. Probe-free;
17	//!   diagnostic instrumentation will re-enter post-`0.6.0`
18	//!   via an actor-wrapper trait or `TProbe::Counter`.
19	//! - **All-in-one (legacy, removed in `0.6.0-3`)**:
20	//!   [`RuntimeZC::run`] (probe-free, returns per-actor
21	//!   counts) and [`RuntimeZC::run_probed`] (probe-
22	//!   instrumented, returns per-actor `(count, TProbe)`).
23	//!   These manage their own warmup → measurement → shutdown
24	//!   internally; the warmup boundary issues `ClearProbe` so
25	//!   counts and the histogram only see steady-state data.
26	//!
27	//! Drain invariant: by the time the join completes, every
28	//! [`PooledMsg`] that was ever in flight has been dropped, so
29	//! `pool.free_len() == pool.size()`. Holds for both API
30	//! styles.
31	
32	use std::sync::mpsc::{self, Receiver, Sender};
33	use std::thread::{self, JoinHandle};
34	use std::time::Duration;
35	
36	use crate::actor_manager::{ActorManager, ActorZC, ContextZC};
37	use crate::pool::{BufRefStore, MutexLifo, Pool, PoolError, PooledMsg};
38	use tprobe::TProbe;
39	
40	/// Internal channel payload.
41	///
42	/// - `User(msg)` — caller-visible traffic.
43	/// - `ClearProbe` — runtime injects at warmup → measurement
44	///   boundary so the report only sees steady-state data.
45	/// - `Shutdown` — runtime injects after the measurement
46	///   window; actor exits its `recv` loop on receipt.
47	enum SignalZC<S: BufRefStore> {
48	    User(PooledMsg<S>),
49	    ClearProbe,
50	    Shutdown,
51	}
52	
53	/// Multi-threaded zerocopy runtime — message-moving
54	/// infrastructure.
55	///
56	/// - Owns the [`Pool<S>`] (cloned cheaply to each actor
57	///   thread).
58	/// - Spawns threads, wires channels, runs the warmup /
59	///   measure / shutdown lifecycle.
60	/// - Knows nothing about "seeds", named lookups, or what's
61	///   being measured. Initial messages come in as a parameter
62	///   to `run` and are delivered after spawn / before warmup.
63	/// - Generic over the store backend `S` so concurrency
64	///   strategies can be swapped and benchmarked. Default
65	///   [`MutexLifo`].
66	pub struct RuntimeZC<S: BufRefStore = MutexLifo> {
67	    pool: Pool<S>,
68	}
69	
70	impl<S: BufRefStore + 'static> RuntimeZC<S> {
71	    /// Build a runtime that draws buffers from `pool`.
72	    ///
73	    /// - The runtime stores a cheap `Arc` clone; the caller
74	    ///   keeps the original handle so it can build initial
75	    ///   messages or inspect (`free_len`, `size`).
76	    pub fn new(pool: Pool<S>) -> Self {
77	        Self { pool }
78	    }
79	
80	    /// Borrow the runtime's pool handle.
81	    pub fn pool(&self) -> &Pool<S> {
82	        &self.pool
83	    }
84	
85	    /// Drain the manager's actors and run with no probe
86	    /// instrumentation. The default measurement primitive.
87	    ///
88	    /// - `initial_messages` are delivered after thread spawn,
89	    ///   before warmup.
90	    /// - Returns per-actor `count` in id order.
91	    /// - Used by the `goalzc-crit` criterion bench so the
92	    ///   measurement is probe-clean. The lesson learned from
93	    ///   `goal2-crit`'s probe contamination: keeping a probe-
94	    ///   free path on the runtime from the start avoids the
95	    ///   trap of a bench that secretly measures `work + tprobe`.
96	    pub fn run(
97	        &mut self,
98	        mgr: &mut ActorManager<S>,
99	        initial_messages: Vec<(u32, PooledMsg<S>)>,
100	        warmup: Duration,
101	        measurement: Duration,
102	        pin_cores: &[usize],
103	    ) -> Vec<u64> {
104	        self.run_inner(
105	            mgr,
106	            initial_messages,
107	            warmup,
108	            measurement,
109	            pin_cores,
110	            actor_loop::<S>,
111	        )
112	    }
113	
114	    /// Same orchestration as [`RuntimeZC::run`], plus a
115	    /// per-message [`TProbe`] in the hot loop.
116	    ///
117	    /// - Each actor thread owns a `TProbe` recording per-
118	    ///   `handle_message` latency at `inner=1`.
119	    /// - Returns `(count, probe)` per actor in id order.
120	    /// - Used by the `goalzc` binary's diagnostic report.
121	    ///   Avoid in benches: probe overhead contaminates the
122	    ///   measurement.
123	    pub fn run_probed(
124	        &mut self,
125	        mgr: &mut ActorManager<S>,
126	        initial_messages: Vec<(u32, PooledMsg<S>)>,
127	        warmup: Duration,
128	        measurement: Duration,
129	        pin_cores: &[usize],
130	    ) -> Vec<(u64, TProbe)> {
131	        self.run_inner(
132	            mgr,
133	            initial_messages,
134	            warmup,
135	            measurement,
136	            pin_cores,
137	            actor_loop_probed::<S>,
138	        )
139	    }
140	
141	    /// Spawn actor threads and deliver initial messages.
142	    /// Shared startup logic for [`RuntimeZC::run`] /
143	    /// [`RuntimeZC::run_probed`] (legacy all-in-one) and
144	    /// [`RuntimeZC::startup`] (lifecycle).
145	    ///
146	    /// - Drains the manager's actors via
147	    ///   [`ActorManager::take_actors`].
148	    /// - Builds one `mpsc` channel per actor; spawns one
149	    ///   thread per actor running `actor_loop`, optionally
150	    ///   pinned via `pin_cores[id % pin_cores.len()]`.
151	    /// - Sends `initial_messages` over the per-actor
152	    ///   senders before returning.
153	    /// - Returns the senders (for `ClearProbe` / `Shutdown`
154	    ///   signaling) and join handles (for the eventual join).
155	    fn spawn_and_seed<R: Send + 'static>(
156	        &self,
157	        mgr: &mut ActorManager<S>,
158	        initial_messages: Vec<(u32, PooledMsg<S>)>,
159	        pin_cores: &[usize],
160	        actor_loop: ActorLoopFn<S, R>,
161	    ) -> (Vec<Sender<SignalZC<S>>>, Vec<JoinHandle<R>>) {
162	        let actors = mgr.take_actors();
163	        let n = actors.len();
164	        let pool = self.pool.clone();
165	        let probe_name_prefix = mgr.probe_name_prefix().to_string();
166	
167	        let mut senders: Vec<Sender<SignalZC<S>>> = Vec::with_capacity(n);
168	        let mut receivers: Vec<Option<Receiver<SignalZC<S>>>> = Vec::with_capacity(n);
169	        for _ in 0..n {
170	            let (tx, rx) = mpsc::channel();
171	            senders.push(tx);
172	            receivers.push(Some(rx));
173	        }
174	
175	        let mut handles: Vec<JoinHandle<R>> = Vec::with_capacity(n);
176	        for (id, actor) in actors.into_iter().enumerate() {
177	            #[allow(clippy::unwrap_used)]
178	            // OK: receivers[id] was set to `Some(...)` in the loop above.
179	            let rx = receivers[id].take().unwrap();
180	            let peers: Vec<Sender<SignalZC<S>>> = senders.to_vec();
181	            let probe_name = format!("{}.actor{}", probe_name_prefix, id);
182	            let pin_target = if pin_cores.is_empty() {
183	                None
184	            } else {
185	                Some(pin_cores[id % pin_cores.len()])
186	            };
187	            let actor_pool = pool.clone();
188	            let h = thread::Builder::new()
189	                .name(format!("actor-zc-{id}"))
190	                .spawn(move || {
191	                    tprobe::pin::pin_current(pin_target);
192	                    actor_loop(actor, rx, peers, actor_pool, probe_name)
193	                })
194	                .expect("failed to spawn actor thread");
195	            handles.push(h);
196	        }
197	
198	        for (dst, msg) in initial_messages {
199	            #[allow(clippy::unwrap_used)]
200	            // OK: receivers are alive (threads just spawned); a send
201	            //   failure indicates an immediate thread panic at startup
202	            //   which the join below surfaces with a clearer message.
203	            senders[dst as usize].send(SignalZC::User(msg)).unwrap();
204	        }
205	
206	        (senders, handles)
207	    }
208	
209	    /// Shared orchestration body for the legacy all-in-one
210	    /// [`RuntimeZC::run`] / [`RuntimeZC::run_probed`].
211	    ///
212	    /// - Calls [`Self::spawn_and_seed`] to spawn threads and
213	    ///   deliver initial messages.
214	    /// - Sleeps `warmup`, sends `ClearProbe` to every actor,
215	    ///   sleeps `measurement`, sends `Shutdown`, drops senders,
216	    ///   joins, returns per-actor results.
217	    /// - Per-thread work is delegated to `actor_loop`, a
218	    ///   function pointer (`ActorLoopFn<S, R>`) so this method
219	    ///   monomorphizes per `R` without the closure / `Fn`-bound
220	    ///   overhead.
221	    fn run_inner<R: Send + 'static>(
222	        &mut self,
223	        mgr: &mut ActorManager<S>,
224	        initial_messages: Vec<(u32, PooledMsg<S>)>,
225	        warmup: Duration,
226	        measurement: Duration,
227	        pin_cores: &[usize],
228	        actor_loop: ActorLoopFn<S, R>,
229	    ) -> Vec<R> {
230	        let (senders, handles) = self.spawn_and_seed(mgr, initial_messages, pin_cores, actor_loop);
231	
232	        thread::sleep(warmup);
233	        for tx in &senders {
234	            let _ = tx.send(SignalZC::ClearProbe);
235	        }
236	
237	        thread::sleep(measurement);
238	        for tx in &senders {
239	            let _ = tx.send(SignalZC::Shutdown);
240	        }
241	        drop(senders);
242	
243	        let mut results = Vec::with_capacity(handles.len());
244	        for h in handles {
245	            results.push(h.join().expect("actor thread panicked"));
246	        }
247	        results
248	    }
249	
250	    /// Spawn actor threads and deliver initial messages,
251	    /// returning a [`Handle`] that owns the actor mesh.
252	    ///
253	    /// - The caller drives the lifecycle from there:
254	    ///   [`Handle::run`] sleeps a run window (any number
255	    ///   of times); [`Handle::stop`] sends `Shutdown`,
256	    ///   joins, and returns the aggregate `u64` count.
257	    /// - No probe support — counts only. Probing re-enters
258	    ///   post-`0.6.0` via an actor-wrapper trait or
259	    ///   `TProbe::Counter`.
260	    /// - On `Drop` without `stop`, the handle does a silent
261	    ///   shutdown (panics on actor threads are swallowed).
262	    pub fn startup(
263	        &mut self,
264	        mgr: &mut ActorManager<S>,
265	        initial_messages: Vec<(u32, PooledMsg<S>)>,
266	        pin_cores: &[usize],
267	    ) -> Handle<S> {
268	        let (senders, handles) =
269	            self.spawn_and_seed(mgr, initial_messages, pin_cores, actor_loop::<S>);
270	        Handle {
271	            senders: Some(senders),
272	            handles: Some(handles),
273	            pool: self.pool.clone(),
274	        }
275	    }
276	}
277	
278	/// Live handle to actor threads spawned by
279	/// [`RuntimeZC::startup`].
280	///
281	/// - Holds the per-actor senders and join handles plus a
282	///   clone of the runtime's pool.
283	/// - [`Handle::run`] sleeps a run window; actor threads continue
284	///   processing messages in the meantime. May be called any
285	///   number of times before `stop`.
286	/// - [`Handle::stop`] sends `Shutdown` to every actor, joins
287	///   all threads, and returns the aggregate `u64` count
288	///   (sum of per-thread local counters, no atomics in the
289	///   hot path).
290	/// - On `Drop` without `stop`, sends `Shutdown` and joins
291	///   silently (panics on actor threads are swallowed; drop
292	///   must not panic-during-drop).
293	pub struct Handle<S: BufRefStore = MutexLifo> {
294	    senders: Option<Vec<Sender<SignalZC<S>>>>,
295	    handles: Option<Vec<JoinHandle<u64>>>,
296	    pool: Pool<S>,
297	}
298	
299	impl<S: BufRefStore> Handle<S> {
300	    /// Sleep for `duration` (a run window). Actor threads
301	    /// continue processing messages while the caller sleeps;
302	    /// counts accumulate in their per-thread local `u64`s.
303	    pub fn run(&self, duration: Duration) {
304	        thread::sleep(duration);
305	    }
306	
307	    /// Borrow the runtime's pool handle (for ad-hoc post-
308	    /// startup `get_msg` / inspection).
309	    pub fn pool(&self) -> &Pool<S> {
310	        &self.pool
311	    }
312	
313	    /// Send `ClearProbe` to every actor thread; on receipt
314	    /// each thread zeros its per-thread message count. Used
315	    /// to mark the warmup → measurement boundary so
316	    /// `stop`'s aggregate excludes warmup messages.
317	    ///
318	    /// - Caller-driven: not implicit. The lifecycle API has
319	    ///   no built-in warmup phase.
320	    /// - Best-effort: if a thread already exited, the send
321	    ///   silently fails. The count of an exited thread is
322	    ///   already captured in its `JoinHandle::Output`.
323	    pub fn reset_count(&self) {
324	        if let Some(senders) = self.senders.as_ref() {
325	            for tx in senders {
326	                let _ = tx.send(SignalZC::ClearProbe);
327	            }
328	        }
329	    }
330	
331	    /// Send `Shutdown` to every actor, join all threads, and
332	    /// return the aggregate per-actor message count.
333	    ///
334	    /// - Panics if any actor thread panicked. To swallow
335	    ///   panics (and skip the count), drop the handle
336	    ///   instead of calling `stop`.
337	    pub fn stop(mut self) -> u64 {
338	        let mut total: u64 = 0;
339	        if let Some(senders) = self.senders.take() {
340	            for tx in &senders {
341	                let _ = tx.send(SignalZC::Shutdown);
342	            }
343	            drop(senders);
344	        }
345	        if let Some(handles) = self.handles.take() {
346	            for h in handles {
347	                total = total.saturating_add(h.join().expect("actor thread panicked"));
348	            }
349	        }
350	        total
351	    }
352	}
353	
354	impl<S: BufRefStore> Drop for Handle<S> {
355	    /// Silent graceful shutdown when [`Handle::stop`] was
356	    /// not called: send `Shutdown`, join all threads,
357	    /// discard counts and any panics.
358	    fn drop(&mut self) {
359	        if let Some(senders) = self.senders.take() {
360	            for tx in &senders {
361	                let _ = tx.send(SignalZC::Shutdown);
362	            }
363	            drop(senders);
364	        }
365	        if let Some(handles) = self.handles.take() {
366	            for h in handles {
367	                let _ = h.join();
368	            }
369	        }
370	    }
371	}
372	
373	/// Function-pointer type for the per-thread actor loop, used
374	/// by [`RuntimeZC::run_inner`]. `fn(...) -> R` is `Copy +
375	/// Send + Sync`, which keeps the orchestration scaffold
376	/// generic without closure / `Fn`-bound complications.
377	type ActorLoopFn<S, R> = fn(
378	    Box<dyn ActorZC<S> + Send>,
379	    Receiver<SignalZC<S>>,
380	    Vec<Sender<SignalZC<S>>>,
381	    Pool<S>,
382	    String,
383	) -> R;
384	
385	/// `ContextZC` impl used inside each actor thread.
386	///
387	/// - Holds borrowed references to the actor's peer senders
388	///   and the shared pool.
389	/// - `get_msg` delegates to [`Pool::get_msg`].
390	/// - `send` ships a `PooledMsg` over the appropriate channel;
391	///   on closed channel the message drops back to the pool.
392	struct MultiCtxZC<'a, S: BufRefStore> {
393	    senders: &'a [Sender<SignalZC<S>>],
394	    pool: &'a Pool<S>,
395	}
396	
397	impl<S: BufRefStore> ContextZC<S> for MultiCtxZC<'_, S> {
398	    fn get_msg(&mut self, size: usize) -> Result<PooledMsg<S>, PoolError> {
399	        self.pool.get_msg(size)
400	    }
401	
402	    fn send(&mut self, dst_id: u32, msg: PooledMsg<S>) {
403	        // Ignored Err contains the un-sent SignalZC::User(msg);
404	        // dropping it returns the buffer to the pool.
405	        let _ = self.senders[dst_id as usize].send(SignalZC::User(msg));
406	    }
407	}
408	
409	/// Per-thread loop with `tprobe` instrumentation.
410	///
411	/// - One scope per `handle_message` (`inner=1`).
412	/// - `Signal::User` records.
413	/// - `Signal::ClearProbe` resets probe + count.
414	/// - `Signal::Shutdown` (or closed channel) exits.
415	fn actor_loop_probed<S: BufRefStore + 'static>(
416	    mut actor: Box<dyn ActorZC<S> + Send>,
417	    rx: Receiver<SignalZC<S>>,
418	    peers: Vec<Sender<SignalZC<S>>>,
419	    pool: Pool<S>,
420	    probe_name: String,
421	) -> (u64, TProbe) {
422	    let mut probe = TProbe::new(&probe_name);
423	    let mut count: u64 = 0;
424	    loop {
425	        match rx.recv() {
426	            Ok(SignalZC::User(msg)) => {
427	                let id = probe.start(0);
428	                let mut ctx = MultiCtxZC {
429	                    senders: &peers,
430	                    pool: &pool,
431	                };
432	                actor.handle_message(&mut ctx, &msg);
433	                probe.end(id);
434	                drop(msg);
435	                count += 1;
436	            }
437	            Ok(SignalZC::ClearProbe) => {
438	                probe.clear();
439	                count = 0;
440	            }
441	            Ok(SignalZC::Shutdown) | Err(_) => break,
442	        }
443	    }
444	    (count, probe)
445	}
446	
447	/// Per-thread loop with no probe involvement.
448	///
449	/// - Identical to [`actor_loop_probed`] minus `probe.start` /
450	///   `probe.end` / `probe.clear`.
451	/// - `_probe_name` is unused but kept in the signature so the
452	///   function pointer matches [`ActorLoopFn`] without
453	///   special-casing.
454	fn actor_loop<S: BufRefStore + 'static>(
455	    mut actor: Box<dyn ActorZC<S> + Send>,
456	    rx: Receiver<SignalZC<S>>,
457	    peers: Vec<Sender<SignalZC<S>>>,
458	    pool: Pool<S>,
459	    _probe_name: String,
460	) -> u64 {
461	    let mut count: u64 = 0;
462	    loop {
463	        match rx.recv() {
464	            Ok(SignalZC::User(msg)) => {
465	                let mut ctx = MultiCtxZC {
466	                    senders: &peers,
467	                    pool: &pool,
468	                };
469	                actor.handle_message(&mut ctx, &msg);
470	                drop(msg);
471	                count += 1;
472	            }
473	            Ok(SignalZC::ClearProbe) => {
474	                count = 0;
475	            }
476	            Ok(SignalZC::Shutdown) | Err(_) => break,
477	        }
478	    }
479	    count
480	}

482	#[cfg(test)]
483	mod tests {
484	    use super::*;
485	    use crate::pool::Pool;
486	
487	    /// Generic ping-pong actor reusable across `S` choices.
488	    /// On each message: get a same-sized reply buffer, send
489	    /// to the configured peer, drop the inbound `PooledMsg`
490	    /// (returns its buffer to the pool).
491	    struct PingPong {
492	        peer: u32,
493	    }
494	
495	    impl<S: BufRefStore> ActorZC<S> for PingPong {
496	        fn handle_message(&mut self, ctx: &mut dyn ContextZC<S>, msg: &[u8]) {
497	            let reply = ctx.get_msg(msg.len()).expect("get_msg should succeed");
498	            ctx.send(self.peer, reply);
499	        }
500	    }
501	
502	    /// Two-actor ping-pong drives traffic and shuts down
503	    /// cleanly via the probe path; both actors record nonzero
504	    /// counts.
505	    #[test]
506	    fn ping_pong_runs_and_shuts_down() {
507	        let pool: Pool = Pool::new(64, 4);
508	        let mut rt = RuntimeZC::new(pool.clone());
509	        let mut mgr = ActorManager::new("test.zc");
510	        let a = mgr.add(PingPong { peer: 1 });
511	        let _b = mgr.add(PingPong { peer: 0 });
512	        let initial = vec![(a, pool.get_msg(8).expect("seed"))];
513	        let results = rt.run_probed(
514	            &mut mgr,
515	            initial,
516	            Duration::from_millis(40),
517	            Duration::from_millis(40),
518	            &[],
519	        );
520	        assert_eq!(results.len(), 2);
521	        let total: u64 = results.iter().map(|(c, _)| *c).sum();
522	        assert!(total > 0, "expected nonzero throughput, got count={total}");
523	    }
524	
525	    /// After `run_probed` returns, every buffer the pool ever
526	    /// handed out has come back. Tests the drain story
527	    /// across the channel-close path.
528	    #[test]
529	    fn pool_is_full_after_shutdown() {
530	        let pool: Pool = Pool::new(64, 4);
531	        let mut rt = RuntimeZC::new(pool.clone());
532	        let mut mgr = ActorManager::new("test.drain");
533	        let a = mgr.add(PingPong { peer: 1 });
534	        let _b = mgr.add(PingPong { peer: 0 });
535	        let initial = vec![(a, pool.get_msg(8).expect("seed"))];
536	        let _ = rt.run_probed(
537	            &mut mgr,
538	            initial,
539	            Duration::from_millis(20),
540	            Duration::from_millis(20),
541	            &[],
542	        );
543	        assert_eq!(
544	            pool.free_len(),
545	            pool.size(),
546	            "all buffers should return to pool after shutdown"
547	        );
548	    }
549	
550	    /// `run` (probe-free default) orchestrates identically
551	    /// and returns per-actor counts. Validates that the
552	    /// probe-clean path runs the same workload.
553	    #[test]
554	    fn run_returns_counts() {
555	        let pool: Pool = Pool::new(64, 4);
556	        let mut rt = RuntimeZC::new(pool.clone());
557	        let mut mgr = ActorManager::new("test.np");
558	        let a = mgr.add(PingPong { peer: 1 });
559	        let _b = mgr.add(PingPong { peer: 0 });
560	        let initial = vec![(a, pool.get_msg(8).expect("seed"))];
561	        let results = rt.run(
562	            &mut mgr,
563	            initial,
564	            Duration::from_millis(20),
565	            Duration::from_millis(20),
566	            &[],
567	        );
568	        assert_eq!(results.len(), 2);
569	        let total: u64 = results.iter().sum();
570	        assert!(total > 0, "expected nonzero throughput, got {total}");
571	        assert_eq!(
572	            pool.free_len(),
573	            pool.size(),
574	            "all buffers should return to pool after probe-free shutdown"
575	        );
576	    }
577	
578	    /// `Runtime::pool` exposes the caller's handle; the
579	    /// caller can build initial messages from it without
580	    /// holding a separate clone.
581	    #[test]
582	    fn runtime_pool_accessor_returns_same_handle() {
583	        let pool: Pool = Pool::new(32, 2);
584	        let rt = RuntimeZC::new(pool.clone());
585	        assert_eq!(rt.pool().msg_size(), 32);
586	        assert_eq!(rt.pool().size(), 2);
587	    }
588	
589	    /// Lifecycle round trip: `startup` → `run` (one run
590	    /// window) → `stop` returns the aggregate count and
591	    /// drains the pool.
592	    #[test]
593	    fn lifecycle_round_trip_returns_total() {
594	        let pool: Pool = Pool::new(64, 4);
595	        let mut rt = RuntimeZC::new(pool.clone());
596	        let mut mgr = ActorManager::new("test.lifecycle");
597	        let a = mgr.add(PingPong { peer: 1 });
598	        let _b = mgr.add(PingPong { peer: 0 });
599	        let initial = vec![(a, pool.get_msg(8).expect("seed"))];
600	        let handle = rt.startup(&mut mgr, initial, &[]);
601	        handle.run(Duration::from_millis(40));
602	        let total = handle.stop();
603	        assert!(total > 0, "expected nonzero total, got {total}");
604	        assert_eq!(
605	            pool.free_len(),
606	            pool.size(),
607	            "all buffers should return to pool after stop"
608	        );
609	    }
610	
611	    /// `Handle::run` may be called multiple times before
612	    /// `stop`; the aggregate count covers all run windows.
613	    #[test]
614	    fn lifecycle_multiple_run_windows_accumulate() {
615	        let pool: Pool = Pool::new(64, 4);
616	        let mut rt = RuntimeZC::new(pool.clone());
617	        let mut mgr = ActorManager::new("test.multi");
618	        let a = mgr.add(PingPong { peer: 1 });
619	        let _b = mgr.add(PingPong { peer: 0 });
620	        let initial = vec![(a, pool.get_msg(8).expect("seed"))];
621	        let handle = rt.startup(&mut mgr, initial, &[]);
622	        handle.run(Duration::from_millis(20));
623	        handle.run(Duration::from_millis(20));
624	        let total = handle.stop();
625	        assert!(total > 0, "expected nonzero total, got {total}");
626	    }
627	
628	    /// `Handle::pool` exposes the runtime's pool handle so
629	    /// callers can build ad-hoc messages post-startup
630	    /// without keeping a separate clone.
631	    #[test]
632	    fn lifecycle_handle_pool_accessor() {
633	        let pool: Pool = Pool::new(32, 4);
634	        let mut rt = RuntimeZC::new(pool.clone());
635	        let mut mgr = ActorManager::new("test.handle.pool");
636	        let a = mgr.add(PingPong { peer: 1 });
637	        let _b = mgr.add(PingPong { peer: 0 });
638	        let initial = vec![(a, pool.get_msg(8).expect("seed"))];
639	        let handle = rt.startup(&mut mgr, initial, &[]);
640	        assert_eq!(handle.pool().msg_size(), 32);
641	        assert_eq!(handle.pool().size(), 4);
642	        handle.run(Duration::from_millis(20));
643	        let _ = handle.stop();
644	    }
645	
646	    /// `reset_count` zeros each actor thread's per-thread
647	    /// count at the warmup → measurement boundary. Smoke
648	    /// test: runtime stays healthy (drains, returns nonzero
649	    /// total for the post-reset run window).
650	    #[test]
651	    fn lifecycle_reset_count_runs_clean() {
652	        let pool: Pool = Pool::new(64, 4);
653	        let mut rt = RuntimeZC::new(pool.clone());
654	        let mut mgr = ActorManager::new("test.reset");
655	        let a = mgr.add(PingPong { peer: 1 });
656	        let _b = mgr.add(PingPong { peer: 0 });
657	        let initial = vec![(a, pool.get_msg(8).expect("seed"))];
658	        let handle = rt.startup(&mut mgr, initial, &[]);
659	        handle.run(Duration::from_millis(20));
660	        handle.reset_count();
661	        handle.run(Duration::from_millis(20));
662	        let total = handle.stop();
663	        assert!(total > 0, "expected nonzero total post-reset, got {total}");
664	        assert_eq!(
665	            pool.free_len(),
666	            pool.size(),
667	            "all buffers should return to pool after stop"
668	        );
669	    }
670	
671	    /// Dropping `Handle` without calling `stop` performs a
672	    /// silent graceful shutdown: actors finish, threads
673	    /// join, pool drains.
674	    #[test]
675	    fn lifecycle_handle_drop_shuts_down_cleanly() {
676	        let pool: Pool = Pool::new(64, 4);
677	        let mut rt = RuntimeZC::new(pool.clone());
678	        let mut mgr = ActorManager::new("test.drop");
679	        let a = mgr.add(PingPong { peer: 1 });
680	        let _b = mgr.add(PingPong { peer: 0 });
681	        let initial = vec![(a, pool.get_msg(8).expect("seed"))];
682	        {
683	            let handle = rt.startup(&mut mgr, initial, &[]);
684	            handle.run(Duration::from_millis(20));
685	            // drop without stop — shutdown happens in Drop.
686	        }
687	        assert_eq!(
688	            pool.free_len(),
689	            pool.size(),
690	            "all buffers should return to pool after drop"
691	        );
692	    }
693	}

1	//! goalzc-crit: criterion smoke benchmark for the lifecycle
2	//! API on the pool-backed payload path.
3	//!
4	//! Drives the same two-thread two-actor zerocopy ping-pong
5	//! workload `goalzc` runs, via the lifecycle API
6	//! ([`RuntimeZC::startup`] → [`Handle::run`] → [`Handle::stop`]).
7	//! Invoked as `cargo bench --bench goalzc-crit` from
8	//! `crates/actor-x1`.
9	//!
10	//! Probe-free by construction (the runtime no longer ships a
11	//! probed path; that re-enters post-`0.6.0` via an actor-
12	//! wrapper trait or `TProbe::Counter`).
13	//!
14	//! Shape note. `Handle::run` is wall-clock driven — actor
15	//! threads ping-pong freely for a caller-supplied run window;
16	//! there is no "do N messages" primitive. To fit criterion's
17	//! iteration-count model, each `iter_custom` call runs a
18	//! short warmup window, calls `Handle::reset_count` to zero
19	//! each thread's per-thread count, runs a fixed measurement
20	//! window, reads the count via `Handle::stop`, then scales
21	//! the reported duration to
22	//! `measurement * iters / measurement_count` so criterion's
23	//! per-iter estimate reflects per-message time during the
24	//! measurement window only. Each iteration is "one
25	//! `handle_message` call at either actor".
26	//! `Throughput::Elements(1)` makes criterion's throughput
27	//! summary read as msgs / s.
28	//!
29	//! Compare the reported per-message time / throughput against
30	//! `goalzc`'s `M msg/s` line at the same `--size` and pinning
31	//! (default: unpinned, `--size 64`).
32	
33	use std::time::Duration;
34	
35	use actor_x1::actor_manager::{ActorManager, ActorZC, ContextZC};
36	use actor_x1::pool::{BufRefStore, Pool};
37	use actor_x1::runtime_zc::RuntimeZC;
38	use criterion::{Criterion, Throughput, criterion_group, criterion_main};
39	
40	/// Bench-local zerocopy ping-pong actor: forwards a same-sized
41	/// reply on each message. Mirrors `goalzc`'s `PingPongZC`.
42	struct PingPongZC {
43	    peer_id: u32,
44	}
45	
46	impl<S: BufRefStore> ActorZC<S> for PingPongZC {
47	    /// Reply with a same-sized buffer to `self.peer_id`. Never blocks.
48	    fn handle_message(&mut self, ctx: &mut dyn ContextZC<S>, msg: &[u8]) {
49	        let reply = ctx.get_msg(msg.len()).expect("get_msg should succeed"); // OK: pool sized for ping-pong steady state (2 in flight; 4 capacity)
50	        ctx.send(self.peer_id, reply);
51	    }
52	}
53	
54	/// Per `iter_custom` call: spin up a fresh pool / runtime /
55	/// manager, warm up briefly, reset counts, measure a fixed
56	/// run window via the lifecycle API, scale to iters.
57	fn bench_goalzc(c: &mut Criterion) {
58	    const SIZE: u32 = 64;
59	    let mut group = c.benchmark_group("goalzc-crit");
60	    group.throughput(Throughput::Elements(1));
61	    // Per sample: 50 ms warmup + 100 ms measurement + thread
62	    // spawn / join (~a few ms) ≈ 160 ms. 50 samples * 160 ms ≈
63	    // 8 s of the sampling phase, plus criterion's own internal
64	    // warmup (a handful of calls).
65	    group.measurement_time(Duration::from_secs(10));
66	    group.sample_size(50);
67	    group.bench_function("pingpong", |b| {
68	        b.iter_custom(|iters| {
69	            let pool: Pool = Pool::new(SIZE, 4);
70	            let mut rt = RuntimeZC::new(pool.clone());
71	            let mut mgr = ActorManager::new("goalzc-crit");
72	            let a_id = mgr.add(PingPongZC { peer_id: 1 });
73	            let _b_id = mgr.add(PingPongZC { peer_id: 0 });
74	            let initial = vec![(
75	                a_id,
76	                pool.get_msg(SIZE as usize).expect("seed get_msg"), // OK: fresh pool with capacity 4 satisfies a single get
77	            )];
78	
79	            let warmup = Duration::from_millis(50);
80	            let measurement = Duration::from_millis(100);
81	            let handle = rt.startup(&mut mgr, initial, &[]);
82	            handle.run(warmup);
83	            handle.reset_count();
84	            handle.run(measurement);
85	            let measurement_count = handle.stop();
86	
87	            // Scale: `measurement` of wall time produced
88	            // `measurement_count` messages (post-reset). Criterion
89	            // wants total time for `iters` iterations, so project
90	            // linearly.
91	            let per_msg_ns = measurement.as_nanos() as u64 / measurement_count.max(1);
92	            Duration::from_nanos(per_msg_ns.saturating_mul(iters))
93	        });
94	    });
95	    group.finish();
96	}
97	
98	criterion_group!(benches, bench_goalzc);
99	criterion_main!(benches);
100	
```

  "old_string": "/// Internal channel payload.\n///\n/// - `User(msg)` — caller-visible traffic.\n/// - `ClearProbe` — runtime injects at warmup → measurement\n///   boundary so the report only sees steady-state data.\n/// - `Shutdown` — runtime injects after the measurement\n///   window; actor exits its `recv` loop on receipt.\nenum SignalZC<S: BufRefStore> {\n    User(PooledMsg<S>),\n    ClearProbe,\n    Shutdown,\n}",
  "new_string": "/// Internal channel payload.\n///\n/// - `User(msg)` — caller-visible traffic.\n/// - `ClearProbe` — runtime injects at warmup → measurement\n///   boundary so the report only sees steady-state data.\n/// - `QueryCount(reply)` — lifecycle injects on\n///   [`Handle::query_count`]; actor sends its current per-\n///   thread count over `reply` and continues running. Probe\n///   state (if any) is untouched.\n/// - `Shutdown` — runtime injects after the measurement\n///   window; actor exits its `recv` loop on receipt.\nenum SignalZC<S: BufRefStore> {\n    User(PooledMsg<S>),\n    ClearProbe,\n    QueryCount(Sender<u64>),\n    Shutdown,\n}"
}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/wink/data/prgs/rust/actor-x1/crates/actor-x1/src/runtime_zc.rs",
  "old_string": "    /// Send `ClearProbe` to every actor thread; on receipt\n    /// each thread zeros its per-thread message count. Used\n    /// to mark the warmup → measurement boundary so\n    /// `stop`'s aggregate excludes warmup messages.\n    ///\n    /// - Caller-driven: not implicit. The lifecycle API has\n    ///   no built-in warmup phase.\n    /// - Best-effort: if a thread already exited, the send\n    ///   silently fails. The count of an exited thread is\n    ///   already captured in its `JoinHandle::Output`.\n    pub fn reset_count(&self) {\n        if let Some(senders) = self.senders.as_ref() {\n            for tx in senders {\n                let _ = tx.send(SignalZC::ClearProbe);\n            }\n        }\n    }",
  "new_string": "    /// Send `ClearProbe` to every actor thread; on receipt\n    /// each thread zeros its per-thread message count. Used\n    /// to mark the warmup → measurement boundary so\n    /// `stop`'s aggregate excludes warmup messages.\n    ///\n    /// - Caller-driven: not implicit. The lifecycle API has\n    ///   no built-in warmup phase.\n    /// - Best-effort: if a thread already exited, the send\n    ///   silently fails. The count of an exited thread is\n    ///   already captured in its `JoinHandle::Output`.\n    pub fn reset_count(&self) {\n        if let Some(senders) = self.senders.as_ref() {\n            for tx in senders {\n                let _ = tx.send(SignalZC::ClearProbe);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Read the current aggregate per-thread count without\n    /// stopping. Sends a `QueryCount(reply)` signal to every\n    /// actor; each thread replies with its current local\n    /// `u64` over the reply channel and continues running.\n    ///\n    /// - Mid-flight: actors keep processing messages between\n    ///   `query_count` and the next call, so two successive\n    ///   reads will not match. The returned value is a\n    ///   point-in-time aggregate, not a snapshot of a stopped\n    ///   world.\n    /// - Best-effort: if a thread already exited (channel\n    ///   closed), its send fails and is skipped. The recv\n    ///   loop only waits on the actors that accepted the\n    ///   query, so a dead thread can't deadlock the caller.\n    /// - Aggregation uses `saturating_add` (matches\n    ///   [`Handle::stop`]).\n    pub fn query_count(&self) -> u64 {\n        let (tx, rx) = mpsc::channel::<u64>();\n        let mut sent: usize = 0;\n        if let Some(senders) = self.senders.as_ref() {\n            for s in senders {\n                if s.send(SignalZC::QueryCount(tx.clone())).is_ok() {\n                    sent += 1;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        drop(tx);\n        let mut total: u64 = 0;\n        for _ in 0..sent {\n            if let Ok(c) = rx.recv() {\n                total = total.saturating_add(c);\n            }\n        }\n        total\n    }"
}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/wink/data/prgs/rust/actor-x1/crates/actor-x1/src/runtime_zc.rs",
  "old_string": "/// Per-thread loop with `tprobe` instrumentation.\n///\n/// - One scope per `handle_message` (`inner=1`).\n/// - `Signal::User` records.\n/// - `Signal::ClearProbe` resets probe + count.\n/// - `Signal::Shutdown` (or closed channel) exits.\nfn actor_loop_probed<S: BufRefStore + 'static>(\n    mut actor: Box<dyn ActorZC<S> + Send>,\n    rx: Receiver<SignalZC<S>>,\n    peers: Vec<Sender<SignalZC<S>>>,\n    pool: Pool<S>,\n    probe_name: String,\n) -> (u64, TProbe) {\n    let mut probe = TProbe::new(&probe_name);\n    let mut count: u64 = 0;\n    loop {\n        match rx.recv() {\n            Ok(SignalZC::User(msg)) => {\n                let id = probe.start(0);\n                let mut ctx = MultiCtxZC {\n                    senders: &peers,\n                    pool: &pool,\n                };\n                actor.handle_message(&mut ctx, &msg);\n                probe.end(id);\n                drop(msg);\n                count += 1;\n            }\n            Ok(SignalZC::ClearProbe) => {\n                probe.clear();\n                count = 0;\n            }\n            Ok(SignalZC::Shutdown) | Err(_) => break,\n        }\n    }\n    (count, probe)\n}",
  "new_string": "/// Per-thread loop with `tprobe` instrumentation.\n///\n/// - One scope per `handle_message` (`inner=1`).\n/// - `Signal::User` records.\n/// - `Signal::ClearProbe` resets probe + count.\n/// - `Signal::QueryCount(reply)` sends current `count` over\n///   `reply` and continues; probe state untouched.\n/// - `Signal::Shutdown` (or closed channel) exits.\nfn actor_loop_probed<S: BufRefStore + 'static>(\n    mut actor: Box<dyn ActorZC<S> + Send>,\n    rx: Receiver<SignalZC<S>>,\n    peers: Vec<Sender<SignalZC<S>>>,\n    pool: Pool<S>,\n    probe_name: String,\n) -> (u64, TProbe) {\n    let mut probe = TProbe::new(&probe_name);\n    let mut count: u64 = 0;\n    loop {\n        match rx.recv() {\n            Ok(SignalZC::User(msg)) => {\n                let id = probe.start(0);\n                let mut ctx = MultiCtxZC {\n                    senders: &peers,\n                    pool: &pool,\n                };\n                actor.handle_message(&mut ctx, &msg);\n                probe.end(id);\n                drop(msg);\n                count += 1;\n            }\n            Ok(SignalZC::ClearProbe) => {\n                probe.clear();\n                count = 0;\n            }\n            Ok(SignalZC::QueryCount(reply)) => {\n                let _ = reply.send(count);\n            }\n            Ok(SignalZC::Shutdown) | Err(_) => break,\n        }\n    }\n    (count, probe)\n}"
}

}

{

  "old_string": "    /// Dropping `Handle` without calling `stop` performs a\n    /// silent graceful shutdown: actors finish, threads\n    /// join, pool drains.\n    #[test]\n    fn lifecycle_handle_drop_shuts_down_cleanly() {",
  "new_string": "    /// `query_count` reads the aggregate per-thread count\n    /// without stopping. Two successive reads across a run\n    /// window are monotonic non-decreasing; `stop`'s total\n    /// is at least the last queried value.\n    #[test]\n    fn lifecycle_query_count_monotonic() {\n        let pool: Pool = Pool::new(64, 4);\n        let mut rt = RuntimeZC::new(pool.clone());\n        let mut mgr = ActorManager::new(\"test.query\");\n        let a = mgr.add(PingPong { peer: 1 });\n        let _b = mgr.add(PingPong { peer: 0 });\n        let initial = vec![(a, pool.get_msg(8).expect(\"seed\"))];\n        let handle = rt.startup(&mut mgr, initial, &[]);\n        handle.run(Duration::from_millis(20));\n        let first = handle.query_count();\n        handle.run(Duration::from_millis(20));\n        let second = handle.query_count();\n        let total = handle.stop();\n        assert!(first > 0, \"expected nonzero first query, got {first}\");\n        assert!(\n            second >= first,\n            \"expected second >= first, got first={first} second={second}\"\n        );\n        assert!(\n            total >= second,\n            \"expected total >= second, got total={total} second={second}\"\n        );\n    }\n\n    /// `query_count` after `reset_count` reflects only the\n    /// post-reset traffic: it is bounded above by `stop`'s\n    /// total and below by zero, and `stop`'s total drops the\n    /// pre-reset window.\n    #[test]\n    fn lifecycle_query_count_after_reset() {\n        let pool: Pool = Pool::new(64, 4);\n        let mut rt = RuntimeZC::new(pool.clone());\n        let mut mgr = ActorManager::new(\"test.query.reset\");\n        let a = mgr.add(PingPong { peer: 1 });\n        let _b = mgr.add(PingPong { peer: 0 });\n        let initial = vec![(a, pool.get_msg(8).expect(\"seed\"))];\n        let handle = rt.startup(&mut mgr, initial, &[]);\n        handle.run(Duration::from_millis(20));\n        handle.reset_count();\n        handle.run(Duration::from_millis(20));\n        let queried = handle.query_count();\n        let total = handle.stop();\n        assert!(queried > 0, \"expected nonzero post-reset query, got {queried}\");\n        assert!(\n            total >= queried,\n            \"expected total >= queried, got total={total} queried={queried}\"\n        );\n    }\n\n    /// Dropping `Handle` without calling `stop` performs a\n    /// silent graceful shutdown: actors finish, threads\n    /// join, pool drains.\n    #[test]\n    fn lifecycle_handle_drop_shuts_down_cleanly() {"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/wink/data/prgs/rust/actor-x1/crates/actor-x1/benches/goalzc-crit.rs",
  "content": "//! goalzc-crit: criterion smoke benchmark for the lifecycle\n//! API on the pool-backed payload path.\n//!\n//! Drives the same two-thread two-actor zerocopy ping-pong\n//! workload `goalzc` runs, via the lifecycle API\n//! ([`RuntimeZC::startup`] → [`Handle::run`] →\n//! [`Handle::reset_count`] / [`Handle::query_count`] →\n//! [`Handle::stop`]). Invoked as\n//! `cargo bench --bench goalzc-crit` from `crates/actor-x1`.\n//!\n//! Probe-free by construction (the runtime no longer ships a\n//! probed path; that re-enters post-`0.6.0` via an actor-\n//! wrapper trait or `TProbe::Counter`).\n//!\n//! Shape note (amortized in `0.6.0-4`). `Handle::run` is\n//! wall-clock driven — actor threads ping-pong freely for a\n//! caller-supplied run window; there is no \"do N messages\"\n//! primitive. To fit criterion's iteration-count model, the\n//! benchmark starts up the actor mesh once outside\n//! `iter_custom`, runs an initial warmup, then each\n//! `iter_custom` call does `reset_count → run(measurement) →\n//! query_count` against the still-live mesh. The per-iter\n//! time is `measurement * iters / measurement_count` so\n//! criterion's per-iter estimate reflects per-message time\n//! during the measurement window only. Each iteration is\n//! \"one `handle_message` call at either actor\".\n//! `Throughput::Elements(1)` makes criterion's throughput\n//! summary read as msgs / s.\n//!\n//! The amortization drops the per-sample\n//! `startup` + thread-spawn + `stop` + join overhead (~a few\n//! ms each); the bench-vs-bin gap from `0.6.0-2` (~11%)\n//! collapses if that overhead was the cause.\n//!\n//! Compare the reported per-message time / throughput against\n//! `goalzc`'s `M msg/s` line at the same `--size` and pinning\n//! (default: unpinned, `--size 64`).\n\nuse std::time::Duration;\n\nuse actor_x1::actor_manager::{ActorManager, ActorZC, ContextZC};\nuse actor_x1::pool::{BufRefStore, Pool};\nuse actor_x1::runtime_zc::RuntimeZC;\nuse criterion::{Criterion, Throughput, criterion_group, criterion_main};\n\n/// Bench-local zerocopy ping-pong actor: forwards a same-sized\n/// reply on each message. Mirrors `goalzc`'s `PingPongZC`.\nstruct PingPongZC {\n    peer_id: u32,\n}\n\nimpl<S: BufRefStore> ActorZC<S> for PingPongZC {\n    /// Reply with a same-sized buffer to `self.peer_id`. Never blocks.\n    fn handle_message(&mut self, ctx: &mut dyn ContextZC<S>, msg: &[u8]) {\n        let reply = ctx.get_msg(msg.len()).expect(\"get_msg should succeed\"); // OK: pool sized for ping-pong steady state (2 in flight; 4 capacity)\n        ctx.send(self.peer_id, reply);\n    }\n}\n\n/// Spin up the actor mesh once, warm up, then per\n/// `iter_custom` call: reset counts, run a fixed window,\n/// query, scale to iters. Handle drops at end of the bench\n/// function (silent shutdown).\nfn bench_goalzc(c: &mut Criterion) {\n    const SIZE: u32 = 64;\n    let mut group = c.benchmark_group(\"goalzc-crit\");\n    group.throughput(Throughput::Elements(1));\n    // Per sample: 100 ms measurement + a `reset_count` +\n    // `query_count` round trip (~µs). 50 samples * ~100 ms ≈\n    // 5 s of sampling phase, plus a one-shot 50 ms warmup\n    // outside `iter_custom`.\n    group.measurement_time(Duration::from_secs(6));\n    group.sample_size(50);\n    group.bench_function(\"pingpong\", |b| {\n        let pool: Pool = Pool::new(SIZE, 4);\n        let mut rt = RuntimeZC::new(pool.clone());\n        let mut mgr = ActorManager::new(\"goalzc-crit\");\n        let a_id = mgr.add(PingPongZC { peer_id: 1 });\n        let _b_id = mgr.add(PingPongZC { peer_id: 0 });\n        let initial = vec![(\n            a_id,\n            pool.get_msg(SIZE as usize).expect(\"seed get_msg\"), // OK: fresh pool with capacity 4 satisfies a single get\n        )];\n        let handle = rt.startup(&mut mgr, initial, &[]);\n        // One-shot warmup so the first sample sees the same\n        // steady state as the rest.\n        handle.run(Duration::from_millis(50));\n\n        b.iter_custom(|iters| {\n            let measurement = Duration::from_millis(100);\n            handle.reset_count();\n            handle.run(measurement);\n            let measurement_count = handle.query_count();\n\n            // Scale: `measurement` of wall time produced\n            // `measurement_count` messages (post-reset).\n            // Criterion wants total time for `iters`\n            // iterations, so project linearly.\n            let per_msg_ns = measurement.as_nanos() as u64 / measurement_count.max(1);\n            Duration::from_nanos(per_msg_ns.saturating_mul(iters))\n        });\n        // `handle` drops here → silent shutdown, pool drains.\n    });\n    group.finish();\n}\n\ncriterion_group!(benches, bench_goalzc);\ncriterion_main!(benches);\n"
}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/wink/data/prgs/rust/actor-x1/notes/chores-03.md",
  "old_string": "### Bin-output policy left in the bins\n\n- `--ticks` / `--decimals` flags (probed bins only).\n- The per-actor `probe.report(...)` call (each bin chooses\n  which probes to render).\n- The throughput line (`goal1: ... messages in ...`) — the\n  bin name is baked into the format string. Promoting that\n  to `report::print_throughput(&str, count, dur_s, ...)`\n  would gain little and lose the literal-grep-ability of\n  the per-bin line.",
  "new_string": "### Bin-output policy left in the bins\n\n- `--ticks` / `--decimals` flags (probed bins only).\n- The per-actor `probe.report(...)` call (each bin chooses\n  which probes to render).\n- The throughput line (`goal1: ... messages in ...`) — the\n  bin name is baked into the format string. Promoting that\n  to `report::print_throughput(&str, count, dur_s, ...)`\n  would gain little and lose the literal-grep-ability of\n  the per-bin line.\n\n## Handle::query_count + amortized goalzc-crit (0.6.0-4)\n\nAdds the signal-and-reply mid-flight count read so\n`goalzc-crit` can amortize bench setup: `startup` once,\nwarm up once, then per criterion sample just\n`reset_count → run(measurement) → query_count`. The\nall-in-one `startup` / thread-spawn / `stop` / join cost\nthat used to live inside `iter_custom` now happens once\nper benchmark group.\n\n- `crates/actor-x1/Cargo.toml`: `0.6.0-3` → `0.6.0-4`.\n- `crates/actor-x1/src/runtime_zc.rs`:\n  - New `SignalZC::QueryCount(Sender<u64>)` variant. On\n    receipt, the actor sends its current per-thread `count`\n    over the reply channel and continues. Probe state (if\n    any, on the legacy probed loop) is untouched.\n  - `actor_loop` and `actor_loop_probed` both handle the\n    new variant. The probed loop's update is functionally\n    dead-on-arrival (legacy paths go away in `0.6.0-6`)\n    but keeps the function-pointer signature uniform.\n  - New `pub fn Handle::query_count(&self) -> u64`. Builds\n    an `mpsc::channel::<u64>()`, sends `QueryCount(tx.clone())`\n    to every actor sender, tracks how many sends succeeded,\n    drops the original `tx`, and receives exactly that many\n    replies. Aggregation uses `saturating_add` (matches\n    [`Handle::stop`]).\n  - Module docstring: lifecycle bullet extended to mention\n    `reset_count` and `query_count`.\n  - New tests: `lifecycle_query_count_monotonic`\n    (`startup → run → query → run → query → stop`; asserts\n    `first > 0`, `second ≥ first`, `total ≥ second`),\n    `lifecycle_query_count_after_reset`\n    (`startup → run → reset → run → query → stop`; asserts\n    `queried > 0` and `total ≥ queried`).\n- `crates/actor-x1/benches/goalzc-crit.rs`:\n  - Pool / runtime / manager / `startup` moved outside\n    `b.iter_custom`. One initial 50 ms warmup happens before\n    sampling starts; the handle drops at the end of the\n    bench function (silent shutdown).\n  - Per `iter_custom` call: `reset_count → run(100 ms) →\n    query_count`. Same `per_msg_ns = measurement /\n    measurement_count` scaling.\n  - `group.measurement_time` reduced 10 s → 6 s. With the\n    per-sample overhead gone (~150 ms → ~100 ms), 50 samples\n    fit in ~5 s of sampling phase plus criterion's internal\n    warmup.\n  - Module docstring rewritten around the amortized shape.\n- `notes/todo.md`: mark `0.6.0-4` done.\n- `notes/chores-03.md`: this section.\n\n### Why best-effort `send` tracking\n\n`Handle::query_count` tracks how many actor sends succeeded\nand only `rx.recv`s that many replies. If an actor thread\nalready exited (channel closed, `send` returns `Err`), its\ncount is unreachable — but skipping its slot in the recv\nloop avoids a deadlock that would otherwise block the\ncaller forever waiting for a reply that's never coming.\nMatches `reset_count`'s best-effort pattern. In practice\nthe handle is alive (pre-`stop`) so all sends succeed; the\ndefensive shape is for symmetry.\n\n### Smoke results (`--size 64`, unpinned)\n\n- `goalzc-crit/pingpong` amortized: ~229 K msg/s (criterion\n  CI [220, 237] K elem/s; criterion reports \"Performance\n  has regressed\", p < 0.05, throughput change −2.6% to\n  −11% vs the `0.6.0-2` non-amortized baseline at ~260 K).\n- Direction is opposite to what the chores-03 prediction\n  expected — the bench-vs-bin gap *widened*, not collapsed.\n  The bot thinks the most likely cause is that the actor\n  threads now run continuously for the full ~6 s sampling\n  phase (50 samples × ~100 ms back-to-back), where the\n  non-amortized variant spun threads up fresh each sample\n  with ~50 ms idle between samples. Sustained two-core\n  load could be hitting thermal / frequency-boost limits\n  that the bursty variant escaped. A pin-to-isolated-cores\n  comparison would be the cheap next experiment; not done\n  here.\n- The regression is an empirical finding worth keeping,\n  not a reason to revert: the amortized API is correct,\n  the lifecycle surface is the right shape for the post-\n  `0.6.0` story, and `query_count` is independently useful\n  beyond benches. The bench number is a measurement, not\n  a goal.\n\n### CLI / runtime surface impact\n\n- Public lifecycle API gains one method (`Handle::query_count`);\n  nothing removed, nothing renamed.\n- `SignalZC` gains one private variant — internal only;\n  no consumer outside `runtime_zc.rs` touches it.\n- No CLI changes."
}
