> AGENT

Work complete on `0.6.0-4`. Summary:

**API additions** (`crates/actor-x1/src/runtime_zc.rs`):
- `SignalZC::QueryCount(Sender<u64>)` — internal signal-and-reply variant.
- Both actor loops handle the new variant: send current `count` on the reply channel, continue running.
- `Handle::query_count(&self) -> u64` — sends `QueryCount` to each actor, receives N replies, returns `saturating_add` aggregate. Best-effort: tracks successful sends to avoid deadlocking on an already-exited thread.
- Module docstring extended to mention `reset_count` / `query_count`.

**Tests added** (35 pass total, 0 fail):
- `lifecycle_query_count_monotonic` — verifies `first > 0`, `second ≥ first`, `total ≥ second`.
- `lifecycle_query_count_after_reset` — verifies `query_count` post-`reset_count` is positive and bounded by `stop`'s total.

**Bench amortization** (`benches/goalzc-crit.rs`):
- `startup` + initial warmup moved outside `iter_custom`; handle drops at end of bench function.
- Per-sample loop is now `reset_count → run(100 ms) → query_count`.
- `measurement_time` 10 s → 6 s (50 samples × ~100 ms + criterion warmup).

**Empirical result**: ~229 K msg/s (CI [220, 237]) — criterion reports "Performance has regressed" −2.6 to −11% vs the `0.6.0-2` non-amortized baseline at ~260 K. The bench-vs-bin gap *widened*, opposite to the prediction in `chores-03`. The bot thinks sustained two-core load across the full ~6 s sampling phase is hitting thermal / frequency-boost limits the bursty non-amortized variant escaped. Documented in the chores section as an empirical finding; not a reason to revert (the API is correct, the surface is the right shape for post-`0.6.0`, `query_count` is independently useful).

**Notes updated**: `notes/todo.md` (marked `-4` done), `notes/chores-03.md` (new `## Handle::query_count + amortized goalzc-crit (0.6.0-4)` section with edits, design rationale, and smoke results).

Please review. On approval I'll prep the commit title and body for a second approval before pushing.

1	//! actor-x1 — an experiment in the actor model in Rust.
2	//!
3	//! See `crates/actor-x1/notes/design.md` for the staged design. Stage 1 provides
4	//! a minimal runtime with two actors that ping-pong an empty
5	//! [`Message`] for a caller-supplied duration; Goal1 runs both
6	//! actors on one thread, Goal2 runs each actor on its own thread.
7	//!
8	//! Public surface today:
9	//! - [`Message`]: the empty unit message Stage 1 actors exchange.
10	//! - [`Actor`]: the single-method actor trait.
11	//! - [`Context`]: the send-side handle passed to each dispatch.
12	//! - [`runtime`]: concrete runtimes (single-thread for Goal1; the
13	//!   multi-thread runtime will land in 0.1.0-3).
14	
15	pub mod actor_manager;
16	pub mod pool;
17	pub mod report;
18	pub mod runtime;
19	pub mod runtime_zc;
20	
21	/// Empty unit-type message exchanged between Stage 1 actors.
22	/// Carries no payload by design — Stage 1's contract is only
23	/// "dispatch happens"; Stage 2 replaces this with a richer type.
24	pub struct Message;
25	
26	/// Actor behavior: handle one [`Message`] at a time, never blocking.
27	///
28	/// The only method is [`Actor::handle_message`]; all outbound
29	/// communication goes through the [`Context`] the runtime supplies.
30	pub trait Actor {
31	    /// Handle a single incoming message. Use `ctx` to emit any
32	    /// resulting sends. Must not block.
33	    fn handle_message(&mut self, ctx: &mut dyn Context, msg: Message);
34	}
35	
36	/// Runtime-supplied send handle passed to each
37	/// [`Actor::handle_message`] call. Implementations differ between
38	/// runtimes: the single-thread runtime writes to a shared queue;
39	/// the (future) multi-thread runtime writes to per-actor channels.
40	pub trait Context {
41	    /// Enqueue `msg` for delivery to the actor identified by
42	    /// `dst_id`. Returns immediately; delivery is the runtime's job.
43	    fn send(&mut self, dst_id: u32, msg: Message);
44	}
45	
```

> AGENT

Before implementing, let me lay out the design choices since the chores entry was high-level:

**Proposed shape**:

1. **Trait name & location**: `pub trait Runtime<S: BufRefStore>` in `runtime_zc.rs`. No naming conflict — legacy types are `SingleThreadRuntime`/`MultiThreadRuntime` (in `runtime.rs`).

2. **Trait surface** (minimal, matches what consumers actually call):
   ```rust
   pub trait Runtime<S: BufRefStore> {
       fn startup(&mut self, mgr: &mut ActorManager<S>, initial: Vec<(u32, PooledMsg<S>)>, pin: &[usize]) -> Handle<S>;
       fn pool(&self) -> &Pool<S>;
   }
   ```
   - `Handle<S>` stays a concrete return type. Making it a trait too adds an associated type with no current consumer needing it — out of scope per CLAUDE.md "don't design for hypothetical future requirements".
   - `RuntimeZC::new(pool)` stays as an inherent constructor (not a trait method).

3. **Inherent methods**: move `startup` and `pool` to **trait-only** (drop the inherent versions). Consumers `use actor_x1::runtime_zc::Runtime;` to bring them in scope — that's what "reach through the trait" means concretely. Without the import, code won't compile, so the seam is real, not nominal. Legacy `run` / `run_probed` stay inherent (they go in `-6`).

4. **`SignalZC::ClearProbe → ClearCount`** rename — internal only, no external impact.

5. **Touched consumers**: `goalzc.rs`, `goalzc-crit.rs`, `runtime_zc.rs` tests — add `use ... Runtime;` import; behavior unchanged.

Sound right, or do you want a different trait shape (e.g., add a `Handle` trait too, or keep methods inherent + duplicate on the trait)?

1	//! Goalzc binary: two zerocopy actors, each on its own thread,
2	//! ping-ponging a pool-backed `PooledMsg` (handler view: `&[u8]`)
3	//! over `std::sync::mpsc` channels for a caller-supplied
4	//! duration in seconds, after an optional warmup window.
5	//! Smoke generator for the pool-backed payload path: prints
6	//! messages-handled / aggregate throughput plus the `pinning:`
7	//! line if `--pin` was set.
8	//!
9	//! No diagnostic instrumentation in this version. Probing
10	//! re-enters post-`0.6.0` via an actor-wrapper trait or
11	//! `TProbe::Counter` (out of scope here).
12	//!
13	//! Compared to goal2 (unit `Message`), goalzc exercises the
14	//! `Pool` + `PooledMsg` path: every dispatch costs one `pool.get`
15	//! plus one drop-back. `--size N` sweeps payload size; pool is
16	//! sized for ping-pong steady state (2 buffers in flight) with
17	//! headroom (4 buffers) so a wakeup race never starves.
18	//!
19	//! Usage: `goalzc --help`
20	
21	use std::time::Duration;
22	
23	use actor_x1::actor_manager::{ActorManager, ActorZC, ContextZC};
24	use actor_x1::pool::{BufRefStore, Pool};
25	use actor_x1::report;
26	use actor_x1::runtime_zc::RuntimeZC;
27	use clap::Parser;
28	use tprobe::fmt_commas;
29	
30	/// Zerocopy ping-pong actor: on every inbound message, fabricate
31	/// a reply of the same length from the pool and forward it to
32	/// the configured peer. Drops the inbound `PooledMsg` after the
33	/// handler returns (runtime-owned), which returns its buffer to
34	/// the pool — so per dispatch we see exactly one pool `get` +
35	/// one pool `put`.
36	struct PingPongZC {
37	    peer_id: u32,
38	}
39	
40	impl<S: BufRefStore> ActorZC<S> for PingPongZC {
41	    /// Reply with a same-sized buffer to `self.peer_id`. Never blocks.
42	    fn handle_message(&mut self, ctx: &mut dyn ContextZC<S>, msg: &[u8]) {
43	        let reply = ctx.get_msg(msg.len()).expect("get_msg should succeed"); // OK: pool sized for ping-pong steady state (2 in flight; 4 capacity)
44	        ctx.send(self.peer_id, reply);
45	    }
46	}
47	
48	/// CLI for goalzc: two-thread two-actor zerocopy ping-pong
49	/// smoke generator. Reports aggregate throughput.
50	#[derive(Parser, Debug)]
51	#[command(
52	    version,
53	    about,
54	    long_about = None,
55	    max_term_width = 80,
56	    before_help = concat!(env!("CARGO_PKG_NAME"), " ", env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION")),
57	)]
58	struct Cli {
59	    /// Measurement window in seconds (runs after warmup).
60	    #[arg(value_parser = report::parse_non_negative_secs)]
61	    duration_s: f64,
62	
63	    /// Warmup duration in seconds before measurement. Runs
64	    /// the same dispatch loop; per-thread counts are zeroed
65	    /// at the warmup → measurement boundary via
66	    /// `Handle::reset_count` so reported throughput reflects
67	    /// only the measurement window.
68	    #[arg(short = 'w', long, default_value_t = 0.5, value_parser = report::parse_non_negative_secs)]
69	    warmup: f64,
70	
71	    /// Message payload size in bytes. Pool is built with this
72	    /// `msg_size`, so `get_msg(size)` calls inside the handler
73	    /// always pass the bound check by construction. Must be
74	    /// `>= 1`.
75	    #[arg(short = 's', long, default_value_t = 64, value_parser = parse_positive_size)]
76	    size: u32,
77	
78	    /// Pin each actor thread to a logical CPU. Accepts a
79	    /// comma-separated / range list; actor `i` pins to
80	    /// `pin[i % pin.len()]`. Examples: `--pin 0,1` pairs two
81	    /// actors to two CPUs; `--pin 0` oversubscribes both onto
82	    /// one core. Tightens stdev by eliminating OS thread
83	    /// migration noise.
84	    #[arg(short = 'p', long)]
85	    pin: Option<String>,
86	}
87	
88	/// `value_parser` helper: parse a positive (>=1) `u32`. Local
89	/// to goalzc — only this bin has a payload-size flag, so it
90	/// doesn't belong in the shared `report` module.
91	fn parse_positive_size(s: &str) -> Result<u32, String> {
92	    let v: u32 = s
93	        .parse()
94	        .map_err(|e: std::num::ParseIntError| e.to_string())?;
95	    if v == 0 {
96	        Err(format!("'{s}' must be >= 1"))
97	    } else {
98	        Ok(v)
99	    }
100	}
101	
102	/// Parse CLI, run two zerocopy ping-pong actors on their own
103	/// threads via the lifecycle API, print aggregate throughput.
104	fn main() {
105	    let cli = Cli::parse();
106	    report::print_banner();
107	
108	    // Pool sized for ping-pong steady state (2 in flight) with
109	    // headroom; `Pool::new(size, 4)` makes the `--size` runtime
110	    // bound auto-satisfied since `pool.msg_size() == size`.
111	    let pool: Pool = Pool::new(cli.size, 4);
112	    let mut rt = RuntimeZC::new(pool.clone());
113	    let mut mgr = ActorManager::new("goalzc.dispatch");
114	    let a_id = mgr.add(PingPongZC { peer_id: 1 });
115	    let b_id = mgr.add(PingPongZC { peer_id: 0 });
116	    assert_eq!((a_id, b_id), (0, 1));
117	
118	    let initial = vec![(
119	        a_id,
120	        pool.get_msg(cli.size as usize).expect("seed get_msg"), // OK: fresh pool with capacity 4 satisfies a single get
121	    )];
122	
123	    let pin_cores = report::parse_pin_cores(cli.pin.as_deref());
124	
125	    let n_actors = 2;
126	    let handle = rt.startup(&mut mgr, initial, &pin_cores);
127	    handle.run(Duration::from_secs_f64(cli.warmup));
128	    handle.reset_count();
129	    handle.run(Duration::from_secs_f64(cli.duration_s));
130	    let total_count = handle.stop();
131	
132	    let mmps = total_count as f64 / cli.duration_s / 1e6;
133	    println!(
134	        "goalzc: {} messages in {:.3}s ({mmps:.3} M msg/s, {n_actors} actors, size={size} B)",
135	        fmt_commas(total_count),
136	        cli.duration_s,
137	        size = cli.size,
138	    );
139	    report::print_pinning(&pin_cores, n_actors);
140	}
141	
```

2	//! infrastructure.
3	//!
4	//! Pairs with [`crate::actor_manager`], which owns the actor
5	//! model surface ([`ActorZC`], [`ContextZC`], [`ActorManager`]).
6	//! This module is one consumer of that surface; future
7	//! transports (M:N scheduler, alternative channel types, …)
8	//! would be peers.
9	//!
10	//! Two API styles in this version:
11	//!
12	//! - **Lifecycle (preferred, new in `0.6.0-1`)**: caller
13	//!   composes [`RuntimeZC::startup`] →
14	//!   [`Handle::run`] (any number of run windows) →
15	//!   [`Handle::stop`]. `stop` returns the aggregate `u64`
16	//!   message count across all actor threads. Probe-free;
17	//!   diagnostic instrumentation will re-enter post-`0.6.0`
18	//!   via an actor-wrapper trait or `TProbe::Counter`.
19	//!   [`Handle::reset_count`] zeros per-thread counts mid-
20	//!   flight (warmup → measurement boundary).
21	//!   [`Handle::query_count`] reads the aggregate without
22	//!   stopping (amortize bench setup across samples).
23	//! - **All-in-one (legacy, removed in `0.6.0-3`)**:
24	//!   [`RuntimeZC::run`] (probe-free, returns per-actor
25	//!   counts) and [`RuntimeZC::run_probed`] (probe-
26	//!   instrumented, returns per-actor `(count, TProbe)`).
27	//!   These manage their own warmup → measurement → shutdown
28	//!   internally; the warmup boundary issues `ClearProbe` so
29	//!   counts and the histogram only see steady-state data.
30	//!
31	//! Drain invariant: by the time the join completes, every
32	//! [`PooledMsg`] that was ever in flight has been dropped, so
33	//! `pool.free_len() == pool.size()`. Holds for both API
34	//! styles.
35	
36	use std::sync::mpsc::{self, Receiver, Sender};
37	use std::thread::{self, JoinHandle};
38	use std::time::Duration;
39	
40	use crate::actor_manager::{ActorManager, ActorZC, ContextZC};
41	use crate::pool::{BufRefStore, MutexLifo, Pool, PoolError, PooledMsg};
42	use tprobe::TProbe;
43	
44	/// Internal channel payload.
45	///
46	/// - `User(msg)` — caller-visible traffic.
47	/// - `ClearProbe` — runtime injects at warmup → measurement
48	///   boundary so the report only sees steady-state data.
49	/// - `QueryCount(reply)` — lifecycle injects on
50	///   [`Handle::query_count`]; actor sends its current per-
51	///   thread count over `reply` and continues running. Probe
52	///   state (if any) is untouched.
53	/// - `Shutdown` — runtime injects after the measurement
54	///   window; actor exits its `recv` loop on receipt.
55	enum SignalZC<S: BufRefStore> {
56	    User(PooledMsg<S>),
57	    ClearProbe,
58	    QueryCount(Sender<u64>),
59	    Shutdown,
60	}
61	
62	/// Multi-threaded zerocopy runtime — message-moving
63	/// infrastructure.
64	///
65	/// - Owns the [`Pool<S>`] (cloned cheaply to each actor
66	///   thread).
67	/// - Spawns threads, wires channels, runs the warmup /
68	///   measure / shutdown lifecycle.
69	/// - Knows nothing about "seeds", named lookups, or what's
70	///   being measured. Initial messages come in as a parameter
71	///   to `run` and are delivered after spawn / before warmup.
72	/// - Generic over the store backend `S` so concurrency
73	///   strategies can be swapped and benchmarked. Default
74	///   [`MutexLifo`].
75	pub struct RuntimeZC<S: BufRefStore = MutexLifo> {
76	    pool: Pool<S>,
77	}
78	
79	impl<S: BufRefStore + 'static> RuntimeZC<S> {
80	    /// Build a runtime that draws buffers from `pool`.
81	    ///
82	    /// - The runtime stores a cheap `Arc` clone; the caller
83	    ///   keeps the original handle so it can build initial
84	    ///   messages or inspect (`free_len`, `size`).
85	    pub fn new(pool: Pool<S>) -> Self {
86	        Self { pool }
87	    }
88	
89	    /// Borrow the runtime's pool handle.
90	    pub fn pool(&self) -> &Pool<S> {
91	        &self.pool
92	    }
93	
94	    /// Drain the manager's actors and run with no probe
95	    /// instrumentation. The default measurement primitive.
96	    ///
97	    /// - `initial_messages` are delivered after thread spawn,
98	    ///   before warmup.
99	    /// - Returns per-actor `count` in id order.
100	    /// - Used by the `goalzc-crit` criterion bench so the
101	    ///   measurement is probe-clean. The lesson learned from
102	    ///   `goal2-crit`'s probe contamination: keeping a probe-
103	    ///   free path on the runtime from the start avoids the
104	    ///   trap of a bench that secretly measures `work + tprobe`.
105	    pub fn run(
106	        &mut self,
107	        mgr: &mut ActorManager<S>,
108	        initial_messages: Vec<(u32, PooledMsg<S>)>,
109	        warmup: Duration,
110	        measurement: Duration,
111	        pin_cores: &[usize],
112	    ) -> Vec<u64> {
113	        self.run_inner(
114	            mgr,
115	            initial_messages,
116	            warmup,
117	            measurement,
118	            pin_cores,
119	            actor_loop::<S>,
120	        )
121	    }
122	
123	    /// Same orchestration as [`RuntimeZC::run`], plus a
124	    /// per-message [`TProbe`] in the hot loop.
125	    ///
126	    /// - Each actor thread owns a `TProbe` recording per-
127	    ///   `handle_message` latency at `inner=1`.
128	    /// - Returns `(count, probe)` per actor in id order.
129	    /// - Used by the `goalzc` binary's diagnostic report.
130	    ///   Avoid in benches: probe overhead contaminates the
```

259	    /// Spawn actor threads and deliver initial messages,
260	    /// returning a [`Handle`] that owns the actor mesh.
261	    ///
262	    /// - The caller drives the lifecycle from there:
263	    ///   [`Handle::run`] sleeps a run window (any number
264	    ///   of times); [`Handle::stop`] sends `Shutdown`,
265	    ///   joins, and returns the aggregate `u64` count.
266	    /// - No probe support — counts only. Probing re-enters
267	    ///   post-`0.6.0` via an actor-wrapper trait or
268	    ///   `TProbe::Counter`.
269	    /// - On `Drop` without `stop`, the handle does a silent
270	    ///   shutdown (panics on actor threads are swallowed).
271	    pub fn startup(
272	        &mut self,
273	        mgr: &mut ActorManager<S>,
274	        initial_messages: Vec<(u32, PooledMsg<S>)>,
275	        pin_cores: &[usize],
276	    ) -> Handle<S> {
277	        let (senders, handles) =
278	            self.spawn_and_seed(mgr, initial_messages, pin_cores, actor_loop::<S>);
279	        Handle {
280	            senders: Some(senders),
281	            handles: Some(handles),
282	            pool: self.pool.clone(),
283	        }
284	    }
285	}
286	
287	/// Live handle to actor threads spawned by
288	/// [`RuntimeZC::startup`].
289	///
290	/// - Holds the per-actor senders and join handles plus a
291	///   clone of the runtime's pool.
292	/// - [`Handle::run`] sleeps a run window; actor threads continue
293	///   processing messages in the meantime. May be called any
294	///   number of times before `stop`.
295	/// - [`Handle::stop`] sends `Shutdown` to every actor, joins
296	///   all threads, and returns the aggregate `u64` count
297	///   (sum of per-thread local counters, no atomics in the
298	///   hot path).
299	/// - On `Drop` without `stop`, sends `Shutdown` and joins
300	///   silently (panics on actor threads are swallowed; drop
301	///   must not panic-during-drop).
302	pub struct Handle<S: BufRefStore = MutexLifo> {
303	    senders: Option<Vec<Sender<SignalZC<S>>>>,
304	    handles: Option<Vec<JoinHandle<u64>>>,
305	    pool: Pool<S>,
306	}
307	
308	impl<S: BufRefStore> Handle<S> {
309	    /// Sleep for `duration` (a run window). Actor threads
310	    /// continue processing messages while the caller sleeps;
311	    /// counts accumulate in their per-thread local `u64`s.
312	    pub fn run(&self, duration: Duration) {
313	        thread::sleep(duration);
314	    }
315	
316	    /// Borrow the runtime's pool handle (for ad-hoc post-
317	    /// startup `get_msg` / inspection).
318	    pub fn pool(&self) -> &Pool<S> {
319	        &self.pool
320	    }
321	
322	    /// Send `ClearProbe` to every actor thread; on receipt
323	    /// each thread zeros its per-thread message count. Used
324	    /// to mark the warmup → measurement boundary so
325	    /// `stop`'s aggregate excludes warmup messages.
326	    ///
327	    /// - Caller-driven: not implicit. The lifecycle API has
328	    ///   no built-in warmup phase.
329	    /// - Best-effort: if a thread already exited, the send
330	    ///   silently fails. The count of an exited thread is
331	    ///   already captured in its `JoinHandle::Output`.
332	    pub fn reset_count(&self) {
333	        if let Some(senders) = self.senders.as_ref() {
334	            for tx in senders {
335	                let _ = tx.send(SignalZC::ClearProbe);
336	            }
337	        }
338	    }
339	
340	    /// Read the current aggregate per-thread count without

342	    /// actor; each thread replies with its current local

344	    ///
345	    /// - Mid-flight: actors keep processing messages between
346	    ///   `query_count` and the next call, so two successive
347	    ///   reads will not match. The returned value is a
348	    ///   point-in-time aggregate, not a snapshot of a stopped
349	    ///   world.
350	    /// - Best-effort: if a thread already exited (channel
351	    ///   closed), its send fails and is skipped. The recv
352	    ///   loop only waits on the actors that accepted the
353	    ///   query, so a dead thread can't deadlock the caller.
354	    /// - Aggregation uses `saturating_add` (matches
355	    ///   [`Handle::stop`]).
356	    pub fn query_count(&self) -> u64 {
357	        let (tx, rx) = mpsc::channel::<u64>();

362	                    sent += 1;
363	                }
364	            }
365	        }
366	        drop(tx);

  "new_string": "}\n\n/// Lifecycle surface for a zerocopy actor runtime.\n///\n/// - `startup` spawns the actor mesh, delivers initial\n///   messages, and returns a [`Handle`] the caller drives\n///   via [`Handle::run`] / [`Handle::reset_count`] /\n///   [`Handle::query_count`] / [`Handle::stop`].\n/// - `pool` borrows the runtime's pool handle (initial-\n///   message construction, `free_len` / `size` inspection).\n/// - Implemented by [`RuntimeZC`]; introduced in `0.6.0-5`\n///   so post-`0.6.0` ActorManager-driven graceful-shutdown\n///   work has a stable seam to drop into. Consumers\n///   (`goalzc`, `goalzc-crit`, lifecycle tests) reach\n///   through this trait rather than the concrete type — an\n///   `import` here is the compile-time guarantee that the\n///   lifecycle surface is the only thing being touched.\n/// - Object-safe (`&self` / `&mut self`, no generic params\n///   on methods) so a future `dyn Runtime<S>` consumer\n///   compiles without additional plumbing.\npub trait Runtime<S: BufRefStore> {\n    /// Spawn actor threads and deliver initial messages,\n    /// returning a [`Handle`] that owns the actor mesh.\n    ///\n    /// - The caller drives the lifecycle from there:\n    ///   [`Handle::run`] sleeps a run window (any number\n    ///   of times); [`Handle::stop`] sends `Shutdown`,\n    ///   joins, and returns the aggregate `u64` count.\n    /// - No probe support — counts only. 223	    /// Shared orchestration body for the legacy all-in-one
224	    /// [`RuntimeZC::run`] / [`RuntimeZC::run_probed`].
225	    ///
226	    /// - Calls [`Self::spawn_and_seed`] to spawn threads and
227	    ///   deliver initial messages.
228	    /// - Sleeps `warmup`, sends `ClearProbe` to every actor,
229	    ///   sleeps `measurement`, sends `Shutdown`, drops senders,
230	    ///   joins, returns per-actor results.
231	    /// - Per-thread work is delegated to `actor_loop`, a
232	    ///   function pointer (`ActorLoopFn<S, R>`) so this method
233	    ///   monomorphizes per `R` without the closure / `Fn`-bound
234	    ///   overhead.
235	    fn run_inner<R: Send + 'static>(
236	        &mut self,
237	        mgr: &mut ActorManager<S>,
238	        initial_messages: Vec<(u32, PooledMsg<S>)>,
239	        warmup: Duration,
240	        measurement: Duration,
241	        pin_cores: &[usize],
242	        actor_loop: ActorLoopFn<S, R>,
243	    ) -> Vec<R> {
244	        let (senders, handles) = self.spawn_and_seed(mgr, initial_messages, pin_cores, actor_loop);
245	
246	        thread::sleep(warmup);
247	        for tx in &senders {
248	            let _ = tx.send(SignalZC::ClearProbe);
249	        }
250	
251	        thread::sleep(measurement);
252	        for tx in &senders {
253	            let _ = tx.send(SignalZC::Shutdown);
254	        }
```

364	    /// Send `ClearProbe` to every actor thread; on receipt
365	    /// each thread zeros its per-thread message count. Used
366	    /// to mark the warmup → measurement boundary so
367	    /// `stop`'s aggregate excludes warmup messages.
368	    ///
369	    /// - Caller-driven: not implicit. The lifecycle API has

372	    ///   silently fails. The count of an exited thread is
373	    ///   already captured in its `JoinHandle::Output`.
374	    pub fn reset_count(&self) {
375	        if let Some(senders) = self.senders.as_ref() {
376	            for tx in senders {
377	                let _ = tx.send(SignalZC::ClearProbe);
378	            }
379	        }
380	    }

496	/// Per-thread loop with `tprobe` instrumentation.
497	///
498	/// - One scope per `handle_message` (`inner=1`).
499	/// - `Signal::User` records.
500	/// - `Signal::ClearProbe` resets probe + count.
501	/// - `Signal::QueryCount(reply)` sends current `count` over
502	///   `reply` and continues; probe state untouched.
503	/// - `Signal::Shutdown` (or closed channel) exits.
504	fn actor_loop_probed<S: BufRefStore + 'static>(
505	    mut actor: Box<dyn ActorZC<S> + Send>,
506	    rx: Receiver<SignalZC<S>>,
507	    peers: Vec<Sender<SignalZC<S>>>,
508	    pool: Pool<S>,
509	    probe_name: String,
510	) -> (u64, TProbe) {
511	    let mut probe = TProbe::new(&probe_name);
512	    let mut count: u64 = 0;
513	    loop {
514	        match rx.recv() {
515	            Ok(SignalZC::User(msg)) => {
516	                let id = probe.start(0);
517	                let mut ctx = MultiCtxZC {
518	                    senders: &peers,
519	                    pool: &pool,
520	                };
521	                actor.handle_message(&mut ctx, &msg);
522	                probe.end(id);
523	                drop(msg);
524	                count += 1;
525	            }
526	            Ok(SignalZC::ClearProbe) => {
527	                probe.clear();
528	                count = 0;
529	            }
530	            Ok(SignalZC::QueryCount(reply)) => {
531	                let _ = reply.send(count);
532	            }
533	            Ok(SignalZC::Shutdown) | Err(_) => break,
534	        }
535	    }
536	    (count, probe)
537	}
538	
539	/// Per-thread loop with no probe involvement.
540	///
541	/// - Identical to [`actor_loop_probed`] minus `probe.start` /
542	///   `probe.end` / `probe.clear`.
543	/// - `_probe_name` is unused but kept in the signature so the
544	///   function pointer matches [`ActorLoopFn`] without
545	///   special-casing.
546	fn actor_loop<S: BufRefStore + 'static>(
547	    mut actor: Box<dyn ActorZC<S> + Send>,
548	    rx: Receiver<SignalZC<S>>,
549	    peers: Vec<Sender<SignalZC<S>>>,
550	    pool: Pool<S>,
551	    _probe_name: String,
552	) -> u64 {
553	    let mut count: u64 = 0;
554	    loop {
555	        match rx.recv() {
556	            Ok(SignalZC::User(msg)) => {
557	                let mut ctx = MultiCtxZC {
558	                    senders: &peers,
559	                    pool: &pool,
560	                };
561	                actor.handle_message(&mut ctx, &msg);
562	                drop(msg);
563	                count += 1;
564	            }
565	            Ok(SignalZC::ClearProbe) => {
566	                count = 0;
567	            }
568	            Ok(SignalZC::QueryCount(reply)) => {
569	                let _ = reply.send(count);

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/wink/data/prgs/rust/actor-x1/notes/chores-03.md",
  "old_string": "### CLI / runtime surface impact\n\n- Public lifecycle API gains one method (`Handle::query_count`);\n  nothing removed, nothing renamed.\n- `SignalZC` gains one private variant — internal only;\n  no consumer outside `runtime_zc.rs` touches it.\n- No CLI changes.",
  "new_string": "### CLI / runtime surface impact\n\n- Public lifecycle API gains one method (`Handle::query_count`);\n  nothing removed, nothing renamed.\n- `SignalZC` gains one private variant — internal only;\n  no consumer outside `runtime_zc.rs` touches it.\n- No CLI changes.\n\n## Runtime trait + ClearProbe → ClearCount rename (0.6.0-5)\n\nIntroduces a `Runtime` trait carrying the lifecycle entry\npoints (`startup` + `pool`) so post-`0.6.0`\nActorManager-driven graceful-shutdown work has a stable\nseam to drop into. `RuntimeZC` impls the trait; the\ninherent `startup` / `pool` methods are dropped — consumers\nreach through the trait via `use\nactor_x1::runtime_zc::Runtime;`. Without the import the\ncode won't compile, so the seam is real, not nominal.\n\nAlso renames `SignalZC::ClearProbe` to `SignalZC::ClearCount`:\nthe runtime is probe-free since `0.6.0-2`, so the old name\nwas misleading. Pure internal rename — the enum is private.\n\n- `crates/actor-x1/Cargo.toml`: `0.6.0-4` → `0.6.0-5`.\n- `crates/actor-x1/src/runtime_zc.rs`:\n  - `pub trait Runtime<S: BufRefStore>` with\n    `fn startup(&mut self, &mut ActorManager<S>, Vec<(u32,\n    PooledMsg<S>)>, &[usize]) -> Handle<S>` and\n    `fn pool(&self) -> &Pool<S>`. Object-safe (`&self` /\n    `&mut self`, no generic params on methods).\n  - `impl<S: BufRefStore + 'static> Runtime<S> for\n    RuntimeZC<S>` — body identical to the prior inherent\n    methods.\n  - Removed the inherent `RuntimeZC::startup` and\n    `RuntimeZC::pool` methods. `RuntimeZC::new` stays\n    inherent (constructor, not on the trait).\n  - `SignalZC::ClearProbe` → `SignalZC::ClearCount`\n    (variant rename + all `tx.send(SignalZC::ClearProbe)`\n    /  pattern-match arms updated). Doc comments\n    referencing the old name updated; the rename history\n    is recorded on the variant's own doc.\n  - Module docstring: lifecycle bullet now points to\n    `Runtime::startup` and documents the trait-import\n    requirement.\n- `crates/actor-x1/src/bin/goalzc.rs`: `use\n  actor_x1::runtime_zc::{Runtime, RuntimeZC};` (added\n  `Runtime`). No other change — `rt.startup(...)` now\n  resolves through the trait impl.\n- `crates/actor-x1/benches/goalzc-crit.rs`: same import\n  change. No other change.\n- `notes/todo.md`: mark `0.6.0-5` done.\n- `notes/chores-03.md`: this section.\n\n### Why drop the inherent methods rather than keep both\n\nKeeping both inherent and trait methods with the same name\nmakes `rt.startup(...)` resolve to the inherent method\n*even when the trait is in scope* (Rust prefers inherent\nover trait when both exist). That defeats the purpose of\n\"consumers reach through the trait\" — the trait would\nbecome a passive descriptor rather than the live dispatch\npath. Dropping the inherent versions forces the trait\nimport, which is exactly the seam future work needs.\n\n### Why `Handle` stays a concrete return type\n\nMaking `Handle` a trait too (with an associated `type\nHandle: Handle<S>` on `Runtime`) would let the runtime\nreturn its own handle type. But: no current or\nshort-horizon consumer needs that flexibility — every\nzerocopy runtime in the codebase or planned for `0.6.x`\nwill use the same per-thread-counter handle shape. Per\nCLAUDE.md: don't design for hypothetical future\nrequirements. If a second handle shape arrives, lift to\nan associated type then.\n\n### Why `Runtime`, not `RuntimeApi` / `Lifecycle` / etc.\n\n- The trait is the canonical lifecycle surface for the\n  zerocopy runtime family; `Runtime` reads at the call\n  site (`use ...::Runtime; rt.startup(...)`) as exactly\n  what it is.\n- No collision with the legacy `runtime` module — that\n  module's types are `SingleThreadRuntime` and\n  `MultiThreadRuntime`, never bare `Runtime`. The\n  legacy types will eventually be replaced by their\n  zerocopy successors anyway (post-`0.6.0` cleanup).\n- `RuntimeApi` adds three syllables without resolving any\n  ambiguity. `Lifecycle` describes the methods but loses\n  \"this is a runtime.\"\n\n### Smoke validation\n\n- `cargo test`: 35 passed (actor-x1) + 18 passed (tprobe).\n  Lifecycle tests still exercise `startup` /\n  `reset_count` / `query_count` / `stop`, now dispatched\n  through the trait impl.\n- `cargo run --release --bin goalzc -- 1 -w 0.3`:\n  234 K msg/s at 64 B unpinned. Normal range for the\n  amortized goalzc path; trait extraction is dispatch-\n  equivalent to the prior inherent methods (single static\n  call site → monomorphized impl method).\n- `goalzc-crit` not re-run for `0.6.0-5` — no codegen\n  difference expected vs `0.6.0-4`."
}
```

- `impl<S> Runtime<S> for RuntimeZC<S>` — bodies identical to the prior inherent methods.
- Inherent `RuntimeZC::startup` and `RuntimeZC::pool` **removed**; the trait is the only path. `RuntimeZC::new` stays inherent (constructor).

- `runtime.rs`'s separate `Signal::ClearProbe` is untouched (different enum, legacy non-zerocopy runtime, out of scope).

**Consumer updates**:
- `crates/actor-x1/src/bin/goalzc.rs`: `use actor_x1::runtime_zc::{Runtime, RuntimeZC};` (added `Runtime`).
- `crates/actor-x1/benches/goalzc-crit.rs`: same import addition.
- Without the trait import, neither would compile — the seam is enforced, not nominal.

- `cargo test`: 35 passed (actor-x1) + 18 passed (tprobe). Lifecycle tests now dispatch through the trait impl.

**Notes updated**: `notes/todo.md` (marked `-5` done), `notes/chores-03.md` (new section with edits, design rationale for "drop inherent" / "Handle concrete" / "name `Runtime`", and smoke validation).

> AGENT

Plan for `0.6.0-6` (per chores-03):

**Deletions in `runtime_zc.rs`**:
- `RuntimeZC::run` (legacy probe-free all-in-one)
- `RuntimeZC::run_probed` (legacy probed all-in-one)
- `run_inner` (only caller: the two above)
- `actor_loop_probed` (only caller: `run_inner` via `actor_loop`)
- `ActorLoopFn` type alias (only used to swap loops in `run_inner`)
- `use tprobe::TProbe;` (only consumer was `actor_loop_probed`)
- 3 legacy tests: `ping_pong_runs_and_shuts_down`, `pool_is_full_after_shutdown`, `run_returns_counts` — behavior is covered by the lifecycle tests.
- `_probe_name` plumbing (computed in `spawn_and_seed`, passed to `actor_loop` and ignored).

**Simplification**:
- `spawn_and_seed` drops its generic `<R>` (only callable shape returns `u64`); hardcodes `actor_loop::<S>` inline. Per CLAUDE.md "don't design for hypothetical future requirements" — if probed re-entry needs a generic again, lift it then.

**Doc**: module docstring drops the "All-in-one (legacy)" bullet — the section is no longer accurate.

Starting now.

1	//! Multi-threaded zerocopy runtime — message-moving
2	//! infrastructure.
3	//!
4	//! Pairs with [`crate::actor_manager`], which owns the actor
5	//! model surface ([`ActorZC`], [`ContextZC`], [`ActorManager`]).
6	//! This module is one consumer of that surface; future
7	//! transports (M:N scheduler, alternative channel types, …)
8	//! would be peers.
9	//!
10	//! Two API styles in this version:
11	//!
12	//! - **Lifecycle (preferred, new in `0.6.0-1`)**: caller
13	//!   composes [`Runtime::startup`] →
14	//!   [`Handle::run`] (any number of run windows) →
15	//!   [`Handle::stop`]. `stop` returns the aggregate `u64`
16	//!   message count across all actor threads. Probe-free;
17	//!   diagnostic instrumentation will re-enter post-`0.6.0`
18	//!   via an actor-wrapper trait or `TProbe::Counter`.
19	//!   [`Handle::reset_count`] zeros per-thread counts mid-
20	//!   flight (warmup → measurement boundary).
21	//!   [`Handle::query_count`] reads the aggregate without
22	//!   stopping (amortize bench setup across samples).
23	//!   Consumers reach through the [`Runtime`] trait (in
24	//!   scope via `use actor_x1::runtime_zc::Runtime;`) — the
25	//!   inherent `startup` / `pool` methods on [`RuntimeZC`]
26	//!   were removed in `0.6.0-5`.
27	//! - **All-in-one (legacy, removed in `0.6.0-6`)**:
28	//!   [`RuntimeZC::run`] (probe-free, returns per-actor
29	//!   counts) and [`RuntimeZC::run_probed`] (probe-
30	//!   instrumented, returns per-actor `(count, TProbe)`).
31	//!   These manage their own warmup → measurement → shutdown
32	//!   internally; the warmup boundary issues `ClearCount` so
33	//!   counts and the histogram only see steady-state data.
34	//!
35	//! Drain invariant: by the time the join completes, every
36	//! [`PooledMsg`] that was ever in flight has been dropped, so
37	//! `pool.free_len() == pool.size()`. Holds for both API
38	//! styles.
39	
40	use std::sync::mpsc::{self, Receiver, Sender};
41	use std::thread::{self, JoinHandle};
42	use std::time::Duration;
43	
44	use crate::actor_manager::{ActorManager, ActorZC, ContextZC};
45	use crate::pool::{BufRefStore, MutexLifo, Pool, PoolError, PooledMsg};
46	use tprobe::TProbe;
47	
48	/// Internal channel payload.
49	///
50	/// - `User(msg)` — caller-visible traffic.
51	/// - `ClearCount` — runtime / [`Handle::reset_count`] injects
52	///   at the warmup → measurement boundary so the count and
53	///   any probe state only see steady-state data. Renamed
54	///   from `ClearProbe` in `0.6.0-5` (the runtime is probe-
55	///   free; the legacy probed loop still clears its probe
56	///   on the same signal until it's removed in `0.6.0-6`).
57	/// - `QueryCount(reply)` — lifecycle injects on
58	///   [`Handle::query_count`]; actor sends its current per-
59	///   thread count over `reply` and continues running. Probe
60	///   state (if any) is untouched.
61	/// - `Shutdown` — runtime injects after the measurement
62	///   window; actor exits its `recv` loop on receipt.
63	enum SignalZC<S: BufRefStore> {
64	    User(PooledMsg<S>),
65	    ClearCount,
66	    QueryCount(Sender<u64>),
67	    Shutdown,
68	}
69	
70	/// Multi-threaded zerocopy runtime — message-moving
71	/// infrastructure.
72	///
73	/// - Owns the [`Pool<S>`] (cloned cheaply to each actor
74	///   thread).
75	/// - Spawns threads, wires channels, runs the warmup /
76	///   measure / shutdown lifecycle.
77	/// - Knows nothing about "seeds", named lookups, or what's
78	///   being measured. Initial messages come in as a parameter
79	///   to `run` and are delivered after spawn / before warmup.
80	/// - Generic over the store backend `S` so concurrency
81	///   strategies can be swapped and benchmarked. Default
82	///   [`MutexLifo`].
83	pub struct RuntimeZC<S: BufRefStore = MutexLifo> {
84	    pool: Pool<S>,
85	}
86	
87	impl<S: BufRefStore + 'static> RuntimeZC<S> {
88	    /// Build a runtime that draws buffers from `pool`.
89	    ///
90	    /// - The runtime stores a cheap `Arc` clone; the caller
91	    ///   keeps the original handle so it can build initial
92	    ///   messages or inspect (`free_len`, `size`).
93	    /// - Constructor is inherent (not on [`Runtime`]); the
94	    ///   trait carries the lifecycle surface only.
95	    pub fn new(pool: Pool<S>) -> Self {
96	        Self { pool }
97	    }
98	
99	    /// Drain the manager's actors and run with no probe
100	    /// instrumentation. The default measurement primitive.
101	    ///
102	    /// - `initial_messages` are delivered after thread spawn,
103	    ///   before warmup.
104	    /// - Returns per-actor `count` in id order.
105	    /// - Used by the `goalzc-crit` criterion bench so the
106	    ///   measurement is probe-clean. The lesson learned from
107	    ///   `goal2-crit`'s probe contamination: keeping a probe-
108	    ///   free path on the runtime from the start avoids the
109	    ///   trap of a bench that secretly measures `work + tprobe`.
110	    pub fn run(
111	        &mut self,
112	        mgr: &mut ActorManager<S>,
113	        initial_messages: Vec<(u32, PooledMsg<S>)>,
114	        warmup: Duration,
115	        measurement: Duration,
116	        pin_cores: &[usize],
117	    ) -> Vec<u64> {
118	        self.run_inner(
119	            mgr,
120	            initial_messages,
121	            warmup,
122	            measurement,
123	            pin_cores,
124	            actor_loop::<S>,
125	        )
126	    }
127	
128	    /// Same orchestration as [`RuntimeZC::run`], plus a
129	    /// per-message [`TProbe`] in the hot loop.
130	    ///
131	    /// - Each actor thread owns a `TProbe` recording per-
132	    ///   `handle_message` latency at `inner=1`.
133	    /// - Returns `(count, probe)` per actor in id order.
134	    /// - Used by the `goalzc` binary's diagnostic report.
135	    ///   Avoid in benches: probe overhead contaminates the
136	    ///   measurement.
137	    pub fn run_probed(
138	        &mut self,
139	        mgr: &mut ActorManager<S>,
140	        initial_messages: Vec<(u32, PooledMsg<S>)>,
141	        warmup: Duration,
142	        measurement: Duration,
143	        pin_cores: &[usize],
144	    ) -> Vec<(u64, TProbe)> {
145	        self.run_inner(
146	            mgr,
147	            initial_messages,
148	            warmup,
149	            measurement,
150	            pin_cores,
151	            actor_loop_probed::<S>,
152	        )
153	    }
154	
155	    /// Spawn actor threads and deliver initial messages.
156	    /// Shared startup logic for [`RuntimeZC::run`] /
157	    /// [`RuntimeZC::run_probed`] (legacy all-in-one) and
158	    /// [`Runtime::startup`] (lifecycle).
159	    ///
160	    /// - Drains the manager's actors via
161	    ///   [`ActorManager::take_actors`].
162	    /// - Builds one `mpsc` channel per actor; spawns one
163	    ///   thread per actor running `actor_loop`, optionally
164	    ///   pinned via `pin_cores[id % pin_cores.len()]`.
165	    /// - Sends `initial_messages` over the per-actor
166	    ///   senders before returning.
167	    /// - Returns the senders (for `ClearCount` / `Shutdown`
168	    ///   signaling) and join handles (for the eventual join).
169	    fn spawn_and_seed<R: Send + 'static>(
170	        &self,
171	        mgr: &mut ActorManager<S>,
172	        initial_messages: Vec<(u32, PooledMsg<S>)>,
173	        pin_cores: &[usize],
174	        actor_loop: ActorLoopFn<S, R>,
175	    ) -> (Vec<Sender<SignalZC<S>>>, Vec<JoinHandle<R>>) {
176	        let actors = mgr.take_actors();
177	        let n = actors.len();
178	        let pool = self.pool.clone();
179	        let probe_name_prefix = mgr.probe_name_prefix().to_string();
180	
181	        let mut senders: Vec<Sender<SignalZC<S>>> = Vec::with_capacity(n);
182	        let mut receivers: Vec<Option<Receiver<SignalZC<S>>>> = Vec::with_capacity(n);
183	        for _ in 0..n {
184	            let (tx, rx) = mpsc::channel();
185	            senders.push(tx);
186	            receivers.push(Some(rx));
187	        }
188	
189	        let mut handles: Vec<JoinHandle<R>> = Vec::with_capacity(n);
190	        for (id, actor) in actors.into_iter().enumerate() {
191	            #[allow(clippy::unwrap_used)]
192	            // OK: receivers[id] was set to `Some(...)` in the loop above.
193	            let rx = receivers[id].take().unwrap();
194	            let peers: Vec<Sender<SignalZC<S>>> = senders.to_vec();
195	            let probe_name = format!("{}.actor{}", probe_name_prefix, id);
196	            let pin_target = if pin_cores.is_empty() {
197	                None
198	            } else {
199	                Some(pin_cores[id % pin_cores.len()])
200	            };
201	            let actor_pool = pool.clone();
202	            let h = thread::Builder::new()
203	                .name(format!("actor-zc-{id}"))
204	                .spawn(move || {
205	                    tprobe::pin::pin_current(pin_target);
206	                    actor_loop(actor, rx, peers, actor_pool, probe_name)
207	                })
208	                .expect("failed to spawn actor thread");
209	            handles.push(h);
210	        }
211	
212	        for (dst, msg) in initial_messages {
213	            #[allow(clippy::unwrap_used)]
214	            // OK: receivers are alive (threads just spawned); a send
215	            //   failure indicates an immediate thread panic at startup
216	            //   which the join below surfaces with a clearer message.
217	            senders[dst as usize].send(SignalZC::User(msg)).unwrap();
218	        }
219	
220	        (senders, handles)
221	    }
222	
223	    /// Shared orchestration body for the legacy all-in-one
224	    /// [`RuntimeZC::run`] / [`RuntimeZC::run_probed`].
225	    ///
226	    /// - Calls [`Self::spawn_and_seed`] to spawn threads and
227	    ///   deliver initial messages.
228	    /// - Sleeps `warmup`, sends `ClearCount` to every actor,
229	    ///   sleeps `measurement`, sends `Shutdown`, drops senders,
230	    ///   joins, returns per-actor results.
```

```
230	    ///   joins, returns per-actor results.
231	    /// - Per-thread work is delegated to `actor_loop`, a
232	    ///   function pointer (`ActorLoopFn<S, R>`) so this method
233	    ///   monomorphizes per `R` without the closure / `Fn`-bound
234	    ///   overhead.
235	    fn run_inner<R: Send + 'static>(
236	        &mut self,
237	        mgr: &mut ActorManager<S>,
238	        initial_messages: Vec<(u32, PooledMsg<S>)>,
239	        warmup: Duration,
240	        measurement: Duration,
241	        pin_cores: &[usize],
242	        actor_loop: ActorLoopFn<S, R>,
243	    ) -> Vec<R> {
244	        let (senders, handles) = self.spawn_and_seed(mgr, initial_messages, pin_cores, actor_loop);
245	
246	        thread::sleep(warmup);
247	        for tx in &senders {
248	            let _ = tx.send(SignalZC::ClearCount);
249	        }
250	
251	        thread::sleep(measurement);
252	        for tx in &senders {
253	            let _ = tx.send(SignalZC::Shutdown);
254	        }
255	        drop(senders);
256	
257	        let mut results = Vec::with_capacity(handles.len());
258	        for h in handles {
259	            results.push(h.join().expect("actor thread panicked"));
260	        }
261	        results
262	    }
263	}
264	
265	/// Lifecycle surface for a zerocopy actor runtime.
266	///
267	/// - `startup` spawns the actor mesh, delivers initial
268	///   messages, and returns a [`Handle`] the caller drives
269	///   via [`Handle::run`] / [`Handle::reset_count`] /
270	///   [`Handle::query_count`] / [`Handle::stop`].
271	/// - `pool` borrows the runtime's pool handle (initial-
272	///   message construction, `free_len` / `size` inspection).
273	/// - Implemented by [`RuntimeZC`]; introduced in `0.6.0-5`
274	///   so post-`0.6.0` ActorManager-driven graceful-shutdown
275	///   work has a stable seam to drop into. Consumers
276	///   (`goalzc`, `goalzc-crit`, lifecycle tests) reach
277	///   through this trait rather than the concrete type — an
278	///   `import` here is the compile-time guarantee that the
279	///   lifecycle surface is the only thing being touched.
280	/// - Object-safe (`&self` / `&mut self`, no generic params
281	///   on methods) so a future `dyn Runtime<S>` consumer
282	///   compiles without additional plumbing.
283	pub trait Runtime<S: BufRefStore> {
284	    /// Spawn actor threads and deliver initial messages,
285	    /// returning a [`Handle`] that owns the actor mesh.
286	    ///
287	    /// - The caller drives the lifecycle from there:
288	    ///   [`Handle::run`] sleeps a run window (any number
289	    ///   of times); [`Handle::stop`] sends `Shutdown`,
290	    ///   joins, and returns the aggregate `u64` count.
291	    /// - No probe support — counts only. Probing re-enters
292	    ///   post-`0.6.0` via an actor-wrapper trait or
293	    ///   `TProbe::Counter`.
294	    /// - On `Drop` without `stop`, the handle does a silent
295	    ///   shutdown (panics on actor threads are swallowed).
296	    fn startup(
297	        &mut self,
298	        mgr: &mut ActorManager<S>,
299	        initial_messages: Vec<(u32, PooledMsg<S>)>,
300	        pin_cores: &[usize],
301	    ) -> Handle<S>;
302	
303	    /// Borrow the runtime's pool handle.
304	    fn pool(&self) -> &Pool<S>;
305	}
306	
307	impl<S: BufRefStore + 'static> Runtime<S> for RuntimeZC<S> {
308	    fn startup(
309	        &mut self,
310	        mgr: &mut ActorManager<S>,
311	        initial_messages: Vec<(u32, PooledMsg<S>)>,
312	        pin_cores: &[usize],
313	    ) -> Handle<S> {
314	        let (senders, handles) =
315	            self.spawn_and_seed(mgr, initial_messages, pin_cores, actor_loop::<S>);
316	        Handle {
317	            senders: Some(senders),
318	            handles: Some(handles),
319	            pool: self.pool.clone(),
320	        }
321	    }
322	
323	    fn pool(&self) -> &Pool<S> {
324	        &self.pool
325	    }
326	}
327	
328	/// Live handle to actor threads spawned by
329	/// [`RuntimeZC::startup`].
330	///
331	/// - Holds the per-actor senders and join handles plus a
332	///   clone of the runtime's pool.
333	/// - [`Handle::run`] sleeps a run window; actor threads continue
334	///   processing messages in the meantime. May be called any
335	///   number of times before `stop`.
336	/// - [`Handle::stop`] sends `Shutdown` to every actor, joins
337	///   all threads, and returns the aggregate `u64` count
338	///   (sum of per-thread local counters, no atomics in the
339	///   hot path).
340	/// - On `Drop` without `stop`, sends `Shutdown` and joins
341	///   silently (panics on actor threads are swallowed; drop
342	///   must not panic-during-drop).
343	pub struct Handle<S: BufRefStore = MutexLifo> {
344	    senders: Option<Vec<Sender<SignalZC<S>>>>,
345	    handles: Option<Vec<JoinHandle<u64>>>,
346	    pool: Pool<S>,
347	}
348	
349	impl<S: BufRefStore> Handle<S> {
350	    /// Sleep for `duration` (a run window). Actor threads
351	    /// continue processing messages while the caller sleeps;
352	    /// counts accumulate in their per-thread local `u64`s.
353	    pub fn run(&self, duration: Duration) {
354	        thread::sleep(duration);
355	    }
356	
357	    /// Borrow the runtime's pool handle (for ad-hoc post-
358	    /// startup `get_msg` / inspection).
359	    pub fn pool(&self) -> &Pool<S> {
360	        &self.pool
361	    }
362	
363	    /// Send `ClearCount` to every actor thread; on receipt
364	    /// each thread zeros its per-thread message count. Used
365	    /// to mark the warmup → measurement boundary so
366	    /// `stop`'s aggregate excludes warmup messages.
367	    ///
368	    /// - Caller-driven: not implicit. The lifecycle API has
369	    ///   no built-in warmup phase.
370	    /// - Best-effort: if a thread already exited, the send
371	    ///   silently fails. The count of an exited thread is
372	    ///   already captured in its `JoinHandle::Output`.
373	    pub fn reset_count(&self) {
374	        if let Some(senders) = self.senders.as_ref() {
375	            for tx in senders {
376	                let _ = tx.send(SignalZC::ClearCount);
377	            }
378	        }
379	    }
380	
381	    /// Read the current aggregate per-thread count without
382	    /// stopping. Sends a `QueryCount(reply)` signal to every
383	    /// actor; each thread replies with its current local
384	    /// `u64` over the reply channel and continues running.
385	    ///
386	    /// - Mid-flight: actors keep processing messages between
387	    ///   `query_count` and the next call, so two successive
388	    ///   reads will not match. The returned value is a
389	    ///   point-in-time aggregate, not a snapshot of a stopped
390	    ///   world.
391	    /// - Best-effort: if a thread already exited (channel
392	    ///   closed), its send fails and is skipped. The recv
393	    ///   loop only waits on the actors that accepted the
394	    ///   query, so a dead thread can't deadlock the caller.
395	    /// - Aggregation uses `saturating_add` (matches
396	    ///   [`Handle::stop`]).
397	    pub fn query_count(&self) -> u64 {
398	        let (tx, rx) = mpsc::channel::<u64>();
399	        let mut sent: usize = 0;
400	        if let Some(senders) = self.senders.as_ref() {
401	            for s in senders {
402	                if s.send(SignalZC::QueryCount(tx.clone())).is_ok() {
403	                    sent += 1;
404	                }
405	            }
406	        }
407	        drop(tx);
408	        let mut total: u64 = 0;
409	        for _ in 0..sent {
410	            if let Ok(c) = rx.recv() {
411	                total = total.saturating_add(c);
412	            }
413	        }
414	        total
415	    }
416	
417	    /// Send `Shutdown` to every actor, join all threads, and
418	    /// return the aggregate per-actor message count.
419	    ///
420	    /// - Panics if any actor thread panicked. To swallow
421	    ///   panics (and skip the count), drop the handle
422	    ///   instead of calling `stop`.
423	    pub fn stop(mut self) -> u64 {
424	        let mut total: u64 = 0;
425	        if let Some(senders) = self.senders.take() {
426	            for tx in &senders {
427	                let _ = tx.send(SignalZC::Shutdown);
428	            }
429	            drop(senders);

439	
440	impl<S: BufRefStore> Drop for Handle<S> {
441	    /// Silent graceful shutdown when [`Handle::stop`] was
442	    /// not called: send `Shutdown`, join all threads,
443	    /// discard counts and any panics.
444	    fn drop(&mut self) {
445	        if let Some(senders) = self.senders.take() {
446	            for tx in &senders {
447	                let _ = tx.send(SignalZC::Shutdown);
448	            }
449	            drop(senders);
450	        }
451	        if let Some(handles) = self.handles.take() {
452	            for h in handles {
453	                let _ = h.join();
454	            }
455	        }
456	    }
457	}
458	
459	/// Function-pointer type for the per-thread actor loop, used
460	/// by [`RuntimeZC::run_inner`]. `fn(...) -> R` is `Copy +
461	/// Send + Sync`, which keeps the orchestration scaffold
462	/// generic without closure / `Fn`-bound complications.
463	type ActorLoopFn<S, R> = fn(
464	    Box<dyn ActorZC<S> + Send>,
465	    Receiver<SignalZC<S>>,
466	    Vec<Sender<SignalZC<S>>>,
467	    Pool<S>,
468	    String,
469	) -> R;
470	
471	/// `ContextZC` impl used inside each actor thread.
472	///
473	/// - Holds borrowed references to the actor's peer senders
474	///   and the shared pool.
475	/// - `get_msg` delegates to [`Pool::get_msg`].
476	/// - `send` ships a `PooledMsg` over the appropriate channel;
477	///   on closed channel the message drops back to the pool.
478	struct MultiCtxZC<'a, S: BufRefStore> {
479	    senders: &'a [Sender<SignalZC<S>>],
480	    pool: &'a Pool<S>,
481	}
482	
483	impl<S: BufRefStore> ContextZC<S> for MultiCtxZC<'_, S> {
484	    fn get_msg(&mut self, size: usize) -> Result<PooledMsg<S>, PoolError> {
485	        self.pool.get_msg(size)
486	    }
487	
488	    fn send(&mut self, dst_id: u32, msg: PooledMsg<S>) {
489	        // Ignored Err contains the un-sent SignalZC::User(msg);
490	        // dropping it returns the buffer to the pool.
491	        let _ = self.senders[dst_id as usize].send(SignalZC::User(msg));
492	    }
493	}
494	
495	/// Per-thread loop with `tprobe` instrumentation.
496	///
497	/// - One scope per `handle_message` (`inner=1`).
498	/// - `Signal::User` records.
499	/// - `Signal::ClearCount` resets probe + count.
500	/// - `Signal::QueryCount(reply)` sends current `count` over
501	///   `reply` and continues; probe state untouched.
502	/// - `Signal::Shutdown` (or closed channel) exits.
503	fn actor_loop_probed<S: BufRefStore + 'static>(
504	    mut actor: Box<dyn ActorZC<S> + Send>,
505	    rx: Receiver<SignalZC<S>>,
506	    peers: Vec<Sender<SignalZC<S>>>,
507	    pool: Pool<S>,
508	    probe_name: String,
509	) -> (u64, TProbe) {
510	    let mut probe = TProbe::new(&probe_name);
511	    let mut count: u64 = 0;
512	    loop {
513	        match rx.recv() {
514	            Ok(SignalZC::User(msg)) => {
515	                let id = probe.start(0);
516	                let mut ctx = MultiCtxZC {
517	                    senders: &peers,
518	                    pool: &pool,
519	                };
520	                actor.handle_message(&mut ctx, &msg);
521	                probe.end(id);
522	                drop(msg);
523	                count += 1;
524	            }
525	            Ok(SignalZC::ClearCount) => {
526	                probe.clear();
527	                count = 0;
528	            }
529	            Ok(SignalZC::QueryCount(reply)) => {
530	                let _ = reply.send(count);
531	            }
532	            Ok(SignalZC::Shutdown) | Err(_) => break,
533	        }
534	    }
535	    (count, probe)
536	}
537	
538	/// Per-thread loop with no probe involvement.
539	///
540	/// - Identical to [`actor_loop_probed`] minus `probe.start` /
541	///   `probe.end` / `probe.clear`.
542	/// - `_probe_name` is unused but kept in the signature so the
543	///   function pointer matches [`ActorLoopFn`] without
544	///   special-casing.
545	fn actor_loop<S: BufRefStore + 'static>(
546	    mut actor: Box<dyn ActorZC<S> + Send>,
547	    rx: Receiver<SignalZC<S>>,
548	    peers: Vec<Sender<SignalZC<S>>>,
549	    pool: Pool<S>,
550	    _probe_name: String,
551	) -> u64 {
552	    let mut count: u64 = 0;
553	    loop {
554	        match rx.recv() {
555	            Ok(SignalZC::User(msg)) => {
556	                let mut ctx = MultiCtxZC {
557	                    senders: &peers,
558	                    pool: &pool,
559	                };
560	                actor.handle_message(&mut ctx, &msg);
561	                drop(msg);
562	                count += 1;
563	            }
564	            Ok(SignalZC::ClearCount) => {
565	                count = 0;
566	            }
567	            Ok(SignalZC::QueryCount(reply)) => {
568	                let _ = reply.send(count);
569	            }
570	            Ok(SignalZC::Shutdown) | Err(_) => break,
571	        }
572	    }
573	    count
574	}

580	
581	    /// Generic ping-pong actor reusable across `S` choices.
582	    /// On each message: get a same-sized reply buffer, send
583	    /// to the configured peer, drop the inbound `PooledMsg`
584	    /// (returns its buffer to the pool).
585	    struct PingPong {
586	        peer: u32,
587	    }
588	
589	    impl<S: BufRefStore> ActorZC<S> for PingPong {
590	        fn handle_message(&mut self, ctx: &mut dyn ContextZC<S>, msg: &[u8]) {
591	            let reply = ctx.get_msg(msg.len()).expect("get_msg should succeed");
592	            ctx.send(self.peer, reply);
593	        }
594	    }
595	
596	    /// Two-actor ping-pong drives traffic and shuts down
597	    /// cleanly via the probe path; both actors record nonzero
598	    /// counts.
599	    #[test]
600	    fn ping_pong_runs_and_shuts_down() {
601	        let pool: Pool = Pool::new(64, 4);
602	        let mut rt = RuntimeZC::new(pool.clone());
603	        let mut mgr = ActorManager::new("test.zc");
604	        let a = mgr.add(PingPong { peer: 1 });
605	        let _b = mgr.add(PingPong { peer: 0 });
606	        let initial = vec![(a, pool.get_msg(8).expect("seed"))];
607	        let results = rt.run_probed(
608	            &mut mgr,
609	            initial,
610	            Duration::from_millis(40),
611	            Duration::from_millis(40),
612	            &[],
613	        );
614	        assert_eq!(results.len(), 2);
615	        let total: u64 = results.iter().map(|(c, _)| *c).sum();
616	        assert!(total > 0, "expected nonzero throughput, got count={total}");
617	    }
618	
619	    /// After `run_probed` returns, every buffer the pool ever
620	    /// handed out has come back. Tests the drain story
621	    /// across the channel-close path.
622	    #[test]
623	    fn pool_is_full_after_shutdown() {

625	        let mut rt = RuntimeZC::new(pool.clone());
626	        let mut mgr = ActorManager::new("test.drain");
627	        let a = mgr.add(PingPong { peer: 1 });
628	        let _b = mgr.add(PingPong { peer: 0 });
629	        let initial = vec![(a, pool.get_msg(8).expect("seed"))];

#[allow(clippy::unwrap_used)]\n            // OK: receivers[id] was set to `Some(...)` in the loop above.\n            let rx = receivers[id].take().unwrap();\n            let peers: Vec<Sender<SignalZC<S>>> = senders.to_vec();\n            let pin_target = if pin_cores.is_empty() {\n                None\n            } else {\n                Some(pin_cores[id % pin_cores.len()])\n            };\n            let actor_pool = pool.clone();\n            let h = thread::Builder::new()\n                .name(format!(\"actor-zc-{id}\"))\n                .spawn(move || {\n                    tprobe::pin::pin_current(pin_target);\n                    actor_loop(actor, rx, peers, actor_pool)\n                })\n                .expect(\"failed to spawn actor thread\");\n            handles.push(h);\n        }\n\n        for (dst, msg) in initial_messages {\n            #[allow(clippy::unwrap_used)]\n            // OK: receivers are alive (threads just spawned); a send\n            //   failure indicates an immediate thread panic at startup\n            //   which the join below surfaces with a clearer message.\n            senders[dst as usize].send(SignalZC::User(msg)).unwrap();\n        }\n\n        (senders, handles)\n    }\n}"
}
```

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/wink/data/prgs/rust/actor-x1/crates/actor-x1/src/runtime_zc.rs",
  "old_string": "/// Per-thread loop with `tprobe` instrumentation.\n///\n/// - One scope per `handle_message` (`inner=1`).\n/// - `Signal::User` records.\n/// - `Signal::ClearCount` resets probe + count.\n/// - `Signal::QueryCount(reply)` sends current `count` over\n///   `reply` and continues; probe state untouched.\n/// - `Signal::Shutdown` (or closed channel) exits.\nfn actor_loop_probed<S: BufRefStore + 'static>(\n    mut actor: Box<dyn ActorZC<S> + Send>,\n    rx: Receiver<SignalZC<S>>,\n    peers: Vec<Sender<SignalZC<S>>>,\n    pool: Pool<S>,\n    probe_name: String,\n) -> (u64, TProbe) {\n    let mut probe = TProbe::new(&probe_name);\n    let mut count: u64 = 0;\n    loop {\n        match rx.recv() {\n            Ok(SignalZC::User(msg)) => {\n                let id = probe.start(0);\n                let mut ctx = MultiCtxZC {\n                    senders: &peers,\n                    pool: &pool,\n                };\n                actor.handle_message(&mut ctx, &msg);\n                probe.end(id);\n                drop(msg);\n                count += 1;\n            }\n            Ok(SignalZC::ClearCount) => {\n                probe.clear();\n                count = 0;\n            }\n            Ok(SignalZC::QueryCount(reply)) => {\n                let _ = reply.send(count);\n            }\n            Ok(SignalZC::Shutdown) | Err(_) => break,\n        }\n    }\n    (count, probe)\n}\n\n/// Per-thread loop with no probe involvement.\n///\n/// - Identical to [`actor_loop_probed`] minus `probe.start` /\n///   `probe.end` / `probe.clear`.\n/// - `_probe_name` is unused but kept in the signature so the\n///   function pointer matches [`ActorLoopFn`] without\n///   special-casing.\nfn actor_loop<S: BufRefStore + 'static>(\n    mut actor: Box<dyn ActorZC<S> + Send>,\n    rx: Receiver<SignalZC<S>>,\n    peers: Vec<Sender<SignalZC<S>>>,\n    pool: Pool<S>,\n    _probe_name: String,\n) -> u64 {\n    let mut count: u64 = 0;\n    loop {\n        match rx.recv() {\n            Ok(SignalZC::User(msg)) => {\n                let mut ctx = MultiCtxZC {\n                    senders: &peers,\n                    pool: &pool,\n                };\n                actor.handle_message(&mut ctx, &msg);\n                drop(msg);\n                count += 1;\n            }\n            Ok(SignalZC::ClearCount) => {\n                count = 0;\n            }\n            Ok(SignalZC::QueryCount(reply)) => {\n                let _ = reply.send(count);\n            }\n            Ok(SignalZC::Shutdown) | Err(_) => break,\n        }\n    }\n    count\n}",
  "new_string": "/// Per-thread actor loop. Returns the per-thread message\n/// count at exit (joined and aggregated by [`Handle::stop`]).\n///\n/// - `Signal::User` invokes [`ActorZC::handle_message`] and\n///   bumps the count.\n/// - `Signal::ClearCount` zeros the count (warmup →\n///   measurement boundary).\n/// - `Signal::QueryCount(reply)` sends the current `count`\n///   over `reply` and continues — mid-flight read.\n/// - `Signal::Shutdown` (or closed channel) exits.\nfn actor_loop<S: BufRefStore + 'static>(\n    mut actor: Box<dyn ActorZC<S> + Send>,\n    rx: Receiver<SignalZC<S>>,\n    peers: Vec<Sender<SignalZC<S>>>,\n    pool: Pool<S>,\n) -> u64 {\n    let mut count: u64 = 0;\n    loop {\n        match rx.recv() {\n            Ok(SignalZC::User(msg)) => {\n                let mut ctx = MultiCtxZC {\n                    senders: &peers,\n                    pool: &pool,\n                };\n                actor.handle_message(&mut ctx, &msg);\n                drop(msg);\n                count += 1;\n            }\n            Ok(SignalZC::ClearCount) => {\n                count = 0;\n            }\n            Ok(SignalZC::QueryCount(reply)) => {\n                let _ = reply.send(count);\n            }\n            Ok(SignalZC::Shutdown) | Err(_) => break,\n        }\n    }\n    count\n}"
}

  "old_string": "//! Two API styles in this version:\n//!\n//! - **Lifecycle (preferred, new in `0.6.0-1`)**: caller\n//!   composes [`Runtime::startup`] →\n//!   [`Handle::run`] (any number of run windows) →\n//!   [`Handle::stop`]. `stop` returns the aggregate `u64`\n//!   message count across all actor threads. Probe-free;\n//!   diagnostic instrumentation will re-enter post-`0.6.0`\n//!   via an actor-wrapper trait or `TProbe::Counter`.\n//!   [`Handle::reset_count`] zeros per-thread counts mid-\n//!   flight (warmup → measurement boundary).\n//!   [`Handle::query_count`] reads the aggregate without\n//!   stopping (amortize bench setup across samples).\n//!   Consumers reach through the [`Runtime`] trait (in\n//!   scope via `use actor_x1::runtime_zc::Runtime;`) — the\n//!   inherent `startup` / `pool` methods on [`RuntimeZC`]\n//!   were removed in `0.6.0-5`.\n//! - **All-in-one (legacy, removed in `0.6.0-6`)**:\n//!   [`RuntimeZC::run`] (probe-free, returns per-actor\n//!   counts) and [`RuntimeZC::run_probed`] (probe-\n//!   instrumented, returns per-actor `(count, TProbe)`).\n//!   These manage their own warmup → measurement → shutdown\n//!   internally; the warmup boundary issues `ClearCount` so\n//!   counts and the histogram only see steady-state data.\n//!\n//! Drain invariant: by the time the join completes, every\n//! [`PooledMsg`] that was ever in flight has been dropped, so\n//! `pool.free_len() == pool.size()`. Holds for both API\n//! styles.",
  "new_string": "//! API surface (lifecycle-only since `0.6.0-6`):\n//!\n//! - Caller composes [`Runtime::startup`] →\n//!   [`Handle::run`] (any number of run windows) →\n//!   [`Handle::stop`]. `stop` returns the aggregate `u64`\n//!   message count across all actor threads. Probe-free;\n//!   diagnostic instrumentation will re-enter post-`0.6.0`\n//!   via an actor-wrapper trait or `TProbe::Counter`.\n//! - [`Handle::reset_count`] zeros per-thread counts mid-\n//!   flight (warmup → measurement boundary).\n//! - [`Handle::query_count`] reads the aggregate without\n//!   stopping (amortize bench setup across samples).\n//! - Consumers reach through the [`Runtime`] trait (in\n//!   scope via `use actor_x1::runtime_zc::Runtime;`) — the\n//!   inherent `startup` / `pool` methods on [`RuntimeZC`]\n//!   were removed in `0.6.0-5`.\n//!\n//! Drain invariant: by the time the join completes, every\n//! [`PooledMsg`] that was ever in flight has been dropped, so\n//! `pool.free_len() == pool.size()`."
}

Renamed\n///   from `ClearProbe` in `0.6.0-5` (the runtime is probe-\n///   free; the legacy probed loop still clears its probe\n///   on the same signal until it's removed in `0.6.0-6`).\n/// - `QueryCount(reply)` — lifecycle injects on\n///   [`Handle::query_count`]; actor sends its current per-\n///   thread count over `reply` and continues running. Probe\n///   state (if any) is untouched.\n/// - `Shutdown` — runtime injects after the measurement\n///   window; actor exits its `recv` loop on receipt.",
  "new_string": "/// Internal channel payload.\n///\n/// - `User(msg)` — caller-visible traffic.\n/// - `ClearCount` — [`Handle::reset_count`] injects at the\n///   warmup → measurement boundary so the aggregate count\n///   only reflects steady-state data.\n/// - `QueryCount(reply)` — [`Handle::query_count`] injects;\n///   actor sends its current per-thread count over `reply`\n///   and continues running.\n/// - `Shutdown` — [`Handle::stop`] / [`Handle::drop`]\n///   injects; actor exits its `recv` loop on receipt."
}

  "old_string": "    /// Two-actor ping-pong drives traffic and shuts down\n    /// cleanly via the probe path; both actors record nonzero\n    /// counts.\n    #[test]\n    fn ping_pong_runs_and_shuts_down() {\n        let pool: Pool = Pool::new(64, 4);\n        let mut rt = RuntimeZC::new(pool.clone());\n        let mut mgr = ActorManager::new(\"test.zc\");\n        let a = mgr.add(PingPong { peer: 1 });\n        let _b = mgr.add(PingPong { peer: 0 });\n        let initial = vec![(a, pool.get_msg(8).expect(\"seed\"))];\n        let results = rt.run_probed(\n            &mut mgr,\n            initial,\n            Duration::from_millis(40),\n            Duration::from_millis(40),\n            &[],\n        );\n        assert_eq!(results.len(), 2);\n        let total: u64 = results.iter().map(|(c, _)| *c).sum();\n        assert!(total > 0, \"expected nonzero throughput, got count={total}\");\n    }\n\n    /// After `run_probed` returns, every buffer the pool ever\n    /// handed out has come back. Tests the drain story\n    /// across the channel-close path.\n    #[test]\n    fn pool_is_full_after_shutdown() {\n        let pool: Pool = Pool::new(64, 4);\n        let mut rt = RuntimeZC::new(pool.clone());\n        let mut mgr = ActorManager::new(\"test.drain\");\n        let a = mgr.add(PingPong { peer: 1 });\n        let _b = mgr.add(PingPong { peer: 0 });\n        let initial = vec![(a, pool.get_msg(8).expect(\"seed\"))];\n        let _ = rt.run_probed(\n            &mut mgr,\n            initial,\n            Duration::from_millis(20),\n            Duration::from_millis(20),\n            &[],\n        );\n        assert_eq!(\n            pool.free_len(),\n            pool.size(),\n            \"all buffers should return to pool after shutdown\"\n        );\n    }\n\n    /// `run` (probe-free default) orchestrates identically\n    /// and returns per-actor counts. Validates that the\n    /// probe-clean path runs the same workload.\n    #[test]\n    fn run_returns_counts() {\n        let pool: Pool = Pool::new(64, 4);\n        let mut rt = RuntimeZC::new(pool.clone());\n        let mut mgr = ActorManager::new(\"test.np\");\n        let a = mgr.add(PingPong { peer: 1 });\n        let _b = mgr.add(PingPong { peer: 0 });\n        let initial = vec![(a, pool.get_msg(8).expect(\"seed\"))];\n        let results = rt.run(\n            &mut mgr,\n            initial,\n            Duration::from_millis(20),\n            Duration::from_millis(20),\n            &[],\n        );\n        assert_eq!(results.len(), 2);\n        let total: u64 = results.iter().sum();\n        assert!(total > 0, \"expected nonzero throughput, got {total}\");\n        assert_eq!(\n            pool.free_len(),\n            pool.size(),\n            \"all buffers should return to pool after probe-free shutdown\"\n        );\n    }\n\n    /// `Runtime::pool` exposes the caller's handle; the",
  "new_string": "    /// `Runtime::pool` exposes the caller's handle; the"
}

> AGENT

Work complete on `0.6.0-6`. Summary:

**Deletions** (`crates/actor-x1/src/runtime_zc.rs`):
- `RuntimeZC::run` (legacy probe-free all-in-one)
- `RuntimeZC::run_probed` (legacy probed all-in-one)
- `RuntimeZC::run_inner` (shared orchestrator)
- `actor_loop_probed` (probed per-thread loop)
- `ActorLoopFn<S, R>` type alias
- `use tprobe::TProbe;` import
- 3 legacy tests (`ping_pong_runs_and_shuts_down`, `pool_is_full_after_shutdown`, `run_returns_counts`) — behaviors already covered by `lifecycle_*` tests.

**Simplifications**:
- `spawn_and_seed`: dropped `<R: Send + 'static>` generic and `actor_loop: ActorLoopFn<S, R>` parameter; hardcodes `actor_loop::<S>` and returns concrete `JoinHandle<u64>`. Dropped `probe_name_prefix` / `probe_name` plumbing.
- `actor_loop`: dropped the `_probe_name` parameter (only existed to match the function-pointer type).
- Module docstring collapsed from two-style framing to a single "API surface (lifecycle-only since `0.6.0-6`)" list.
- `SignalZC::ClearCount` doc trimmed (no more legacy-probed-loop clause); `Shutdown` doc points at `Handle::stop` / `Handle::drop`.

**Verification** (full checklist this time):
1. ✅ `cargo fmt`
2. ✅ `cargo clippy --all-targets -- -D warnings`
3. ✅ `cargo test`: 32 passed actor-x1 + 18 passed tprobe (the −3 vs `-5` is the removed legacy tests; all 7 lifecycle tests still pass)
4. ✅ `cargo install --path crates/actor-x1`: all 3 bins replaced at `0.6.0-6`
5. ✅ Smoke run: `goalzc 0.5 -w 0.2` → 226 K msg/s at 64 B unpinned, normal range
6. ✅ `notes/todo.md` updated
7. ✅ `notes/chores-03.md` updated (with design rationale for "drop generic" and "drop tests not rewrite")

After this, `RuntimeZC` exposes only `new` (inherent) + the `Runtime` trait surface. Module is lifecycle-only, probe-free, ~120 lines lighter.

Please review. On approval I'll prep the commit title and body.

> DEVELOPER

We need some documenation on for these traits and how to use them, also I like

> DEVELOPER

We need some documenation on for these traits and how to use them, also I like "P" stats for goalzc so we see the rang, mean and adj mean for it too. Thoughts?

> AGENT

Two thoughts, kept brief:

**Docs**: real gap. My recommendation is a worked example in the module docstring (rustdoc-renderable, ~12 lines: `Pool` → `RuntimeZC::new` → `ActorManager` + actors → `startup` → `run` → `reset_count` → `run` → `query_count`/`stop`) plus a `crates/actor-x1/notes/lifecycle.md` design note for the deeper rationale (why `Handle` is concrete not a trait, why drop is silent, why per-thread `u64` instead of atomics, when to use `query_count` vs `stop`). The rustdoc covers "how to use"; the notes file covers "why this shape." Both land naturally in the `0.6.0` close commit.

**P-stats**: I read "range / mean / adj mean" as wanting the same band-table report `goal1`/`goal2` produce — which means re-introducing per-message probing in `goalzc`. The chores-03 plan explicitly deferred this to post-`0.6.0` ("wrapper-trait or `TProbe::Counter`"). My recommendation is a fresh `0.6.1` ladder, *not* squeezing it into `0.6.0` close: the close commit's job is to cap the lifecycle ladder, and adding probing in the same commit would walk back the "runtime is probe-free" line `-6` just settled. Tradeoff: one more close-then-open commit pair instead of bundling.

Concretely: close `0.6.0` with just the docs and the version bump, then open `0.6.1-0` as the plan marker for the probed-goalzc work. Want me to take that next, or would you rather skip the close commit and roll docs + probing into one ladder?

  "new_string": "//! Drain invariant: by the time the join completes, every\n//! [`PooledMsg`] that was ever in flight has been dropped, so\n//! `pool.free_len() == pool.size()`.\n//!\n//! # Worked example\n//!\n//! Two-actor ping-pong with a 50 ms warmup and 100 ms\n//! measurement window. The `Runtime` import brings the\n//! lifecycle methods (`startup` / `pool`) into scope.\n//!\n//! ```no_run\n//! use std::time::Duration;\n//!\n//! use actor_x1::actor_manager::{ActorManager, ActorZC, ContextZC};\n//! use actor_x1::pool::{BufRefStore, Pool};\n//! use actor_x1::runtime_zc::{Runtime, RuntimeZC};\n//!\n//! struct Echo { peer: u32 }\n//! impl<S: BufRefStore> ActorZC<S> for Echo {\n//!     fn handle_message(&mut self, ctx: &mut dyn ContextZC<S>, msg: &[u8]) {\n//!         let reply = ctx.get_msg(msg.len()).expect(\"pool sized for steady state\");\n//!         ctx.send(self.peer, reply);\n//!     }\n//! }\n//!\n//! let pool: Pool = Pool::new(64, 4);\n//! let mut rt = RuntimeZC::new(pool.clone());\n//! let mut mgr = ActorManager::new(\"docs.lifecycle\");\n//! let a = mgr.add(Echo { peer: 1 });\n//! let _b = mgr.add(Echo { peer: 0 });\n//! let initial = vec![(a, pool.get_msg(8).expect(\"seed get_msg\"))];\n//!\n//! let handle = rt.startup(&mut mgr, initial, &[]);\n//! handle.run(Duration::from_millis(50));       // warmup\n//! handle.reset_count();                        // zero per-thread counts\n//! handle.run(Duration::from_millis(100));      // measurement\n//! let total = handle.stop();                   // join + aggregate\n//! println!(\"{total} messages handled in 100 ms\");\n//! ```\n//!\n//! See [`crates/actor-x1/notes/lifecycle.md`] for the design\n//! rationale behind each piece — counter shape, why `Handle`\n//! is concrete, when to choose `query_count` over `stop`,\n//! and the `Drop` contract.\n//!\n//! [`crates/actor-x1/notes/lifecycle.md`]: https://github.com/winksaville/actor-x1/blob/main/crates/actor-x1/notes/lifecycle.md"
}
